M11 · Kun · 1377249652ce

内置指令全景：从注册到执行回执

一个内置指令如何被解析、路由、执行并写回会话？

第 11 / 11 章4 条源码 findingmaster
本章路线问题→比喻→多条源码证据→四问→源码目录→交互图→逐行翻译
先用一个生活比喻

指令像机场值机单：先认出航班，再检查登机规则，交给正确柜台执行，最后把登机和到达状态写回系统。

先回答本章问题

一个内置指令如何被解析、路由、执行并写回会话？ 把 Kun 想成一张任务白板：每个节点都有输入、权限、状态和交付物，线条表示谁把结果交给谁。

本章证据板：4 条 finding

不要把一段代码当成整个系统；下面每张卡分别说明事实、白话、工程影响、边界和可点击的固定源码。

01
L1factarchitecture

01 · 架构与所有权

源码事实

Direct mode 与 Agent Graph 共享 workflow runtime；extension-api 把 Agent、Tool、Permission、RunEvent 对外固化成 schema。

白话解释

像看一栋楼的管线图：入口、控制室、执行层和档案室各有职责，连线告诉你谁把什么交给谁。

对自研 Harness 的含义

把这条实现放回完整任务链，检查输入、状态、副作用、回执和恢复出口。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 1–12
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 13–25
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

docs/workflow-loop.md:1–25固定提交 ↗
    1  # Loop 循环节点 —— 让 agent 自己转圈，而不是你按回车
    2
    3  > 适用于「创建loop / Workflows」里的 **Loop（循环）节点**。
    4  > 实现在 [`src/main/workflow-runtime.ts`](../src/main/workflow-runtime.ts)（`executeNode` 的 `case 'loop'`），配置归一化在 [`src/shared/app-settings-workflow.ts`](../src/shared/app-settings-workflow.ts)，测试在 [`src/main/workflow-runtime.nodes.test.ts`](../src/main/workflow-runtime.nodes.test.ts)。
    5
    6  ---
    7
    8  ## 先理清三个容易混的名字
    9
   10  | 名字 | 是什么 |
   11  | --- | --- |
   12  | **创建loop（Create Loop）** | **一整个工作流**本身。产品里一个工作流就叫一个「创建loop」。 |
   13  | **Loop（循环）节点** | 工作流里的**一个节点**，把另一个「创建loop」当成**循环体反复执行**。本文讲的就是它。 |
   14  | **运行创建loop（子工作流）节点** | 把另一个「创建loop」**只跑一次**（不循环、不反馈）。 |
   15
   16  一句话区分：**子工作流 = 跑一次；Loop 节点 = 反复跑 + 把结果折回去（或对一组元素各跑一遍）。**
   17
   18  ---
   19
   20  ## 核心思想：循环驱动 agent，而不是人驱动 agent
   21
   22  传统的对话式 agent 是这样的：
   23
   24  ```
   25  你打字 → agent 回答 → 你读 → 你决定下一步 → 你再打字 → …
   26  ```
   27
   28  注意循环在哪儿：**循环在「你」身上**。每一轮都靠你坐在那儿、读完结果、按回车把它推到下一轮。agent 只是被你一次次手动喂的那只手。
   29
   30  Loop 节点把这件事**反过来**。你不再一轮一轮地催，而是**一次性声明三件事**：
   31
   32  1. **每轮要干的活（目标）** —— 循环体，本身是一个完整的「创建loop」，里面可以有 AI 节点、条件判断、HTTP、代码等任意节点。
   33  2. **每轮的输出怎么喂回下一轮** —— 上一轮 output 自动变成下一轮 input。
   34  3. **什么时候算完** —— 一个停止条件，外加 `maxIterations` 作为兜底硬顶。
   35
   36  声明完，循环就**自己转**：agent 在「干活 → 看结果 → 再干活」的圈里自走，不需要你在每一轮按回车。
   37
   38  > **一句话：你设目标 + 每轮的活 + 何时算完，剩下交给循环。**
   39
   40  这正是把你本来要**手动重复输入**的那些催促——“再试一次”“现在把 lint 错误修了”“一直跑到测试过”——**一次写成规则，让它无人值守地跑**。这也是整个功能为什么叫「创建loop」：核心赌注就是，agent 工作的未来形态是**声明一个循环（目标 + 循环体 + 停止条件）**，而不是守着聊天框一轮一轮地敲。
   41
   42  ### 为什么"迭代"才是 agent 真正的形状
   43
   44  - 单次 prompt → 单次回答，是**问答**。
   45  - 把输出折回输入、直到收敛，是**迭代**。
   46
   47  agent 真正干活时几乎都是迭代：草稿 → 自审 → 改稿；跑 → 报错 → 修；调工具 → 不满足 → 再调。Loop 节点把"迭代"变成画布上的**一等公民**——而且因为循环体是一个完整工作流，**"一轮"可以任意复杂**，不是只重复一个节点。
   48
   49  ---
   50
   51  ## 两种循环形态
   52
   53  Loop 节点有两个 `mode`，对应两种完全不同的迭代意图。
   54
   55  ### 1. 条件循环（Condition / loop-agent）—— 反复打磨直到达标
   56
   57  意图：**“一直干到目标满足”**。每一轮把循环体的输出当作下一轮的输入，满足停止条件就停；否则一直到 `maxIterations` 兜底停。
   58
   59  - **停止条件**：`leftExpr` / `operator` / `rightValue` / `caseSensitive`，复用 condition 节点那一套判断。默认是 `json.done` `equals` `true`。
   60  - **反馈**：上一轮 `output` → 下一轮 `payload`，所以循环体能在上一轮基础上继续推进。
   61  - **输出**：循环体最后一轮的 json，外加两个元字段——
   62    - `_iterations`：实际跑了几轮；
   63    - `_done`：`true` = 停止条件达成；`false` = 没达成、是被 `maxIterations` 兜底截停的。
   64  - **节点 message**：`looped N (done)` 或 `looped N (max)`。
   65
   66  这就是 **loop-agent**：像一个人不断 refine 直到自己满意，只是把"满意"交给停止条件来判定，把"再来一轮"交给循环来推动。
   67
   68  > 例：**研究循环**。循环体 = 一个 AI 节点（读资料、补充结论、自评是否够了），停止条件 = `json.enough == true`，`maxIterations = 6`。agent 自己读、自己判断够不够、不够就再来一轮，够了或到 6 轮就停。
   69
   70  ### 2. 遍历数组（For each）—— 同样的活，对每个元素干一遍
   71
   72  意图：**“对列表里每个元素，各跑一遍循环体”**。这是**批处理**，不是打磨——元素之间互不反馈。
   73
   74  - **数组来源**：`arraySource` 表达式；留空则用上游 `payload.json`（要求是数组）。
   75  - **执行方式**：`sequential`（一个接一个）/ `parallel`（同时跑，`concurrency` 并发 1–8）。**并行也保证输出顺序与输入一致。**
   76  - **出错策略**：`continueOnError`
   77    - 打开：某个元素出错就把它记成 `{ error: "..." }`、跳过、继续跑其余元素（**韧性**）；
   78    - 关闭（默认）：一旦有元素出错，立即中止、整个 Loop 节点报错（**fail-fast**）。
   79  - **输出**：每个元素结果组成的数组。**节点 message**：`foreach 成功数/总数`（并行时带 `(parallel)`）。
   80
   81  > 例：对 50 个文件**各跑一遍** “AI 总结”，`parallel`、`concurrency = 4`、`continueOnError = on`，最后拿到 50 条结果（出错的那条是 `{error}`）。
   82
   83  ---
   84
   85  ## 循环体里能用的变量：`$loop`
   86
   87  循环体里**任意节点**都能用 `{{ }}` 插值引用当前这一轮的上下文：
   88
   89  | 变量 | 含义 |
   90  | --- | --- |
   91  | `{{$loop.index}}` | 当前轮 / 当前元素的下标（从 0 开始） |
   92  | `{{$loop.item}}` | 当前元素（foreach）；条件循环里是**上一轮输出的 json** |
   93  | `{{$loop.total}}` | 总数（foreach = 数组长度；条件循环 = `maxIterations`） |
   94
   95  ---
   96

02
L1facttools

02 · 工具注册与执行

源码事实

ToolInvocation、progress、result 和 cancellation token 形成工具回执协议，工具不只是一个返回字符串的函数。

白话解释

工具像机场跑道：模型拿到登机牌还不够，注册、权限、参数、执行和回执要逐关通过。

对自研 Harness 的含义

把这条实现放回完整任务链，检查输入、状态、副作用、回执和恢复出口。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 13–26
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 27–42
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

L1factconnectors

03 · 连接器、插件与 MCP

源码事实

把 Kun 想成一张任务白板：每个节点都有输入、权限、状态和交付物，线条表示谁把结果交给谁。

白话解释

把项目想成一间工作室：我们先找入口、工位、工具柜和交付台，不急着背 API 名称。

对自研 Harness 的含义

把这条实现放回完整任务链，检查输入、状态、副作用、回执和恢复出口。

04
L1factinstructions

04 · 全部指令与扩展面

源码事实

AgentProfileDeclaration、extension visibility、tools.register 和权限声明共同构成插件/指令面，调用者必须声明需要什么。

白话解释

把项目想成一间工作室：我们先找入口、工位、工具柜和交付台，不急着背 API 名称。

对自研 Harness 的含义

把这条实现放回完整任务链，检查输入、状态、副作用、回执和恢复出口。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 11–24
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 25–40
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

   20    return Math.max(1, Math.min(60, raw))
   21  }
   22
   23  export function createBackgroundShellTool(options: BackgroundShellToolOptions = {}): LocalTool {
   24    return LocalToolHost.defineTool({
   25      name: 'background_shell',
   26      description:
   27        'Manage shell sessions started with bash background=true in the current thread. The bash tool assigns an 8-character session_id when starting a background command; use that id here. action="list" lists this thread\'s running sessions by default (set include_finished=true to also show completed/stopped/failed sessions). action="read" returns a non-blocking output snapshot. action="poll" waits up to yield_seconds for more output or exit. action="write" sends stdin via input. action="stop" terminates a running session.',
   28      inputSchema: {
   29        type: 'object',
   30        properties: {
   31          action: {
   32            type: 'string',
   33            enum: ['list', 'read', 'poll', 'write', 'stop']
   34          },
   35          session_id: {
   36            type: 'string',
   37            description: 'Required for read, poll, write, and stop. The 8-character id returned by bash when background=true.'
   38          },
   39          yield_seconds: { type: 'number' },
   40          include_finished: { type: 'boolean', default: false },
   41          // Kept only to tolerate old model transcripts. Listing is always
   42          // scoped to the invoking thread; false must never expose other
   43          // threads' commands, working directories, output, or log paths.
   44          thread_only: { type: 'boolean', default: true },
   45          input: { type: 'string' }
   46        },
   47        required: ['action'],
   48        additionalProperties: false
   49      },
   50      policy: 'auto',
   51      // `write` sends arbitrary stdin to a live shell, which can execute a
   52      // command even if the shell was created before a sandbox-policy change.
   53      // Keep the whole multipurpose tool in the command-execution class until
   54      // read-only observation actions are split into a separate tool.
   55      toolKind: 'command_execution',
   56      effects: {
   57        network: true,
   58        externalWrite: true,
   59        processExecution: true,
   60        guiAutomation: false
   61      },
   62      execute: async (args, context) =>
   63        withToolBoundary(async () => {
   64          const action = typeof args.action === 'string' ? args.action.trim() : ''
   65          if (action === 'list') {
   66            let sessions = options.listBackgroundSessions
   67              ? [...options.listBackgroundSessions(context.threadId)]
   68              : await listBashSessionRecords(context.threadId)
   69            if (args.include_finished !== true) {
   70              sessions = sessions.filter((session) => session.status === 'running')
   71            }
   72            return {
   73              output: {
   74                sessions: sessions.map((session) => ({
   75                  session_id: session.id,
   76                  command: session.command,
   77                  cwd: session.cwd,
   78                  shell: session.shell,
   79                  status: session.status,
   80                  started_at: session.startedAt,
   81                  ...(session.finishedAt ? { finished_at: session.finishedAt } : {}),
   82                  exit_code: session.exitCode,
   83                  output: session.output,
   84                  ...(session.outputTruncated ? { output_truncated: true } : {}),

Return combined stdout and stderr. Commands use a runtime-owned 24-hour ceiling. Runs synchronously by default (background defaults to false). Set background=true to start a detached session that keeps running after the turn ends; the tool assigns an 8-character session_id in the response.   931      },

  933      toolKind: 'command_execution',

  935        network: true,
  936        externalWrite: true,
  937        processExecution: true,
  938        guiAutomation: false
  939      },
  940      execute: async (args, context, onUpdate) => withToolBoundary(async () => {
  941        const command = typeof args.command === 'string' ? args.command : ''
  942        if (!command.trim()) return { output: { error: 'command is required' }, isError: true }
  943        const timeout = normalizePositiveInteger(
  944          options.defaultTimeoutSeconds,
  945          DEFAULT_BASH_TIMEOUT_SECONDS
  946        )
  947        const background = args.background === true
  948        const cwd = workspaceRoot(context.workspace)
  949        try {
  950          if (background) {
  951            if (timeout > backgroundLimits.maxTimeoutSeconds) {
  952              return {
  953                output: {
  954                  error: `background shell timeout exceeds ${backgroundLimits.maxTimeoutSeconds} seconds`,
  955                  timeout
  956                },
  957                isError: true
  958              }
  959            }
  960            if (bashOps?.exec) {
  961              return {
  962                output: { error: 'background sessions are not supported with custom bash exec operations' },
  963                isError: true
  964              }
  965            }
  966            const result = await startBackgroundBashSession(
  967              {
  968                command,
  969                cwd,
  970                threadId: context.threadId,
  971                turnId: context.turnId,
  972                signal: context.abortSignal,
  973                timeoutSeconds: timeout,
  974                detached: true,
  975                dataDir: backgroundShellDataDir,
  976                outputLimits,
  977                backgroundLimits
  978              },
  979              shellHooks,
  980              onUpdate,

  196            {
  197              retryable: true,
  198              ...summarizeBrowserUseActionValidation(args)
  199            }
  200          )
  201        }
  202        const action = parsed.data

  209            approvalGrant.toolName !== 'browser_use' ||

  212          )
  213        ) {
  214          return toolError(
  215            'approval_proof_invalid',
  216            'browser_use did not receive a matching one-call Kun approval grant'
  217          )
  218        }
  219        const interaction = ['click', 'type', 'select', 'press'].includes(action.action)
  220        const budget = interaction ? interactionsByTurn : observationsByTurn
  221        const key = `${context.threadId}:${context.turnId}`
  222        const used = budget.get(key) ?? 0
  223        const limit = interaction
  224          ? config.maxInteractionActionsPerTurn
  225          : config.maxObservationActionsPerTurn
  226        if (action.action !== 'close' && used >= limit) {
  227          return toolError(
  228            'action_budget_exhausted',
  229            `browser_use ${interaction ? 'interaction' : 'observation'} limit (${limit}) reached for this turn`

  188        const action = typeof args.action === 'string' ? args.action : ''
  189        if (!action) return toolError('invalid_action', 'action is required')
  190
  191        const turnKey = `${context.threadId}:${context.turnId}`
  192        const used = actionsByTurn.get(turnKey) ?? 0
  193        if (used >= maxActionsPerTurn) {
  194          return toolError(
  195            'action_budget_exhausted',
  196            `reached the computer_use action limit (${maxActionsPerTurn}) for this turn; summarize progress or ask the user how to proceed`
  197          )
  198        }
  199        actionsByTurn.set(turnKey, used + 1)
  200        if (actionsByTurn.size > 64) {
  201          // Evict the oldest OTHER turn — never the active turn, or its budget
  202          // would silently reset mid-run and defeat the runaway backstop.
  203          for (const key of actionsByTurn.keys()) {
  204            if (key !== turnKey) {
  205              actionsByTurn.delete(key)
  206              break
  207            }
  208          }
  209        }
  210
  211        if (context.abortSignal.aborted) {
  212          return toolError('aborted', 'the turn was cancelled before this action ran')
  213        }
  214
  215        const ready = await controller.ensureReady()
  216        if (!ready.available) {
  217          return toolError('computer_use_unavailable', ready.reason ?? 'computer-use backend is unavailable')
  218        }
  219
  220        const coordinate = readCoordinate(args.coordinate)
  221        try {
  222          switch (action) {
  223            case 'screenshot':
  224              return screenshotOutput('screenshot', await controller.capture())
  225
  226            case 'cursor_position': {
  227              const pos = await controller.cursorPosition()
  228              const size = await controller.screenSize()
  229              return {
  230                output: {
  231                  kind: 'computer_action',
  232                  action,
  233                  cursor: [pos.x, pos.y],
  234                  screen: size
  235                }
  236              }
  237            }
  238
  239            case 'mouse_move': {
  240              if (!coordinate) return toolError('missing_coordinate', 'mouse_move requires coordinate [x,y]')

   76            if (useExistingAgents && customAgentSupplied) {
   77              return toolError('custom_agent is unavailable while "Use existing agents" is turned on; select a reusable profile or omit profile for automatic routing')
   78            }
   79            if (!useExistingAgents) {
   80              if (requestedProfile) {
   81                return toolError('profile is unavailable while "Use existing agents" is turned off; define custom_agent instead')
   82              }
   83              if (!customAgentSupplied) {
   84                return toolError('custom_agent is required while "Use existing agents" is turned off')
   85              }
   86            }
   87            if (!useExistingAgents && customAgentSupplied) {
   88              const customDefinition = parseCustomAgent(args.custom_agent)
   89              if (customDefinition instanceof Error) return toolError(customDefinition.message)
   90              if (!customDefinition) return toolError('custom_agent is required')
   91              inlineProfile = {
   92                id: customSubagentProfileId(customDefinition.name),
   93                profile: customSubagentProfile(customDefinition),
   94                source: 'custom'
   95              }
   96              routing = explicitCustomMetadata(inlineProfile, agentSurface)
   97            }
   98
   99            if (useExistingAgents && requestedProfile) {
  100              const snapshot = await runtime.resolveProfileSnapshot(requestedProfile, common.workspace, agentSurface)
  101              if (!snapshot) {
  102                return toolError(`unknown or unavailable ${agentSurface} subagent profile: ${requestedProfile}`)
  103              }
  104              inlineProfile = { id: snapshot.id, profile: snapshot.profile, source: snapshot.source }
  105              routing = {
打开这条指令的动态执行图 →

  198  export function buildDesignToolProtocolManifest(): DesignToolProtocolManifest {
  199    return {
  200      version: 1,
  201      kind: 'kun.design.tool-protocol',
  202      source: 'kun-design-mode',
  203      tools: DESIGN_TOOL_PROTOCOL_TOOLS
  204    }
  205  }
  206
  207  export function designToolProtocolSummaryLines(
  208    tools: readonly DesignToolProtocolTool[] = DESIGN_TOOL_PROTOCOL_TOOLS
  209  ): string[] {
  210    return tools.map((tool) => {
  211      const flags = [
  212        tool.requiresSelection ? 'selection required' : '',
  213        tool.requiresCodeBinding ? 'code binding required' : ''
  214      ].filter(Boolean)
  215      const suffix = flags.length > 0 ? `; ${flags.join('; ')}` : ''
  216      return `- ${tool.id} (${tool.category}): ${tool.purpose}${suffix}`
  217    })
  218  }
  219
  220  export function designToolProtocolById(
  221    id: string,
  222    tools: readonly DesignToolProtocolTool[] = DESIGN_TOOL_PROTOCOL_TOOLS
  223  ): DesignToolProtocolTool | undefined {
  224    return tools.find((tool) => tool.id === id)
  225  }
  226
  227  function unsupportedToolResult(invocation: DesignToolInvocation): DesignToolInvocationResult {
  228    const tool = designToolProtocolById(invocation.toolId)
  229    return {
  230      ok: false,
  231      toolId: invocation.toolId,
  232      status: 'unsupported',
  233      affectedIds: [],

  206
  207  export function designToolProtocolSummaryLines(
  208    tools: readonly DesignToolProtocolTool[] = DESIGN_TOOL_PROTOCOL_TOOLS
  209  ): string[] {
  210    return tools.map((tool) => {
  211      const flags = [
  212        tool.requiresSelection ? 'selection required' : '',
  213        tool.requiresCodeBinding ? 'code binding required' : ''
  214      ].filter(Boolean)
  215      const suffix = flags.length > 0 ? `; ${flags.join('; ')}` : ''

  220  export function designToolProtocolById(

  225  }
  226
  227  function unsupportedToolResult(invocation: DesignToolInvocation): DesignToolInvocationResult {
  228    const tool = designToolProtocolById(invocation.toolId)
  229    return {
  230      ok: false,
  231      toolId: invocation.toolId,
  232      status: 'unsupported',
  233      affectedIds: [],
  234      errors: [{
  235        code: 'UNSUPPORTED_TOOL',
  236        message: tool
  237          ? `${invocation.toolId} is declared but does not have a local executor yet.`
  238          : `Unknown design tool: ${invocation.toolId}`
  239      }],
  240      summaryLines: [
  241        tool
  242          ? `${invocation.toolId}: executor pending`
  243          : `${invocation.toolId}: unknown design tool`
  244      ]
  245    }
  246  }

  217    })

  225  }

展开逐行源码证据
  171  export function createEditLocalTool(_options: EditLocalToolOptions = {}): LocalTool {
  172    const readFileOp = _options.operations?.readFile ?? defaultEditLocalToolOperations.readFile!
  173    const writeFileOp = _options.operations?.writeFile ?? defaultEditLocalToolOperations.writeFile!
  174    const openExternalOp = _options.operations?.openExternal ?? open
  175    return LocalToolHost.defineTool({
  176      name: 'edit',
  177      description: 'Edit a workspace file using exact text replacement. Supports multiple disjoint edits in one call.',
  178      inputSchema: {
  179        type: 'object',
  180        properties: {
  181          path: { type: 'string' },
  182          oldText: { type: 'string' },
  183          newText: { type: 'string' },
  184          edits: {
  185            type: 'array',
  186            items: {
  187              type: 'object',
  188              properties: {
  189                oldText: { type: 'string' },
  190                newText: { type: 'string' }
  191              },
  192              required: ['oldText', 'newText'],
  193              additionalProperties: false
  194            }
  195          }
  196        },
  197        required: ['path'],
  198        additionalProperties: false
  199      },
  200      policy: 'on-request',
  201      toolKind: 'file_change',
  202      externalWritePathArguments: ['path'],
  203      execute: async (args, context) => withToolBoundary(async () => {
  204        const truncated = truncatedArgumentsError(args.__raw)
  205        if (truncated) return truncated
  206        const rawPath = typeof args.path === 'string' ? args.path : ''
  207        const edits = parseEditInstructions(args)
  208        if (!rawPath.trim() || edits.length === 0) {
  209          return { output: { error: 'path and at least one edit are required' }, isError: true }
  210        }
  211        const { absolutePath, relativePath } = await resolveWorkspacePath(rawPath, context)
  212        assertCanWritePath(absolutePath, context)
  213        return withFileMutationQueue(absolutePath, async () => {
  214          const externalTarget = approvedExternalTarget(absolutePath, context)
  215          const handle = externalTarget
  216            ? await openVerifiedExternalTarget(externalTarget, 'edit', openExternalOp)
  217            : undefined
  218          try {
  219            const rawSource = handle
  220              ? await handle.readFile({ encoding: 'utf8' })
  221              : await readFileOp(absolutePath)
  222            const { bom, text: source } = stripBom(rawSource)
  223            const lineEnding = detectLineEnding(source)
  224            const normalizedSource = normalizeToLF(source)
  225            const { baseContent, newContent } = applyEditsToNormalizedContent(normalizedSource, edits, relativePath)
  226            const next = bom + restoreLineEndings(newContent, lineEnding)
  227            if (handle) {
  228              if (!externalTarget) throw new Error('external edit handle is missing its approved target')
  229              await writeTextToHandle(handle, next, externalTarget)
  230            } else {
  231              await writeFileOp(absolutePath, next)
  232            }
  233            const diff = generateDisplayDiff(baseContent, newContent)
  234            const patch = generateUnifiedPatch(relativePath, baseContent, newContent)
  235            return {

  300  function stringValue(value: unknown): string {
  301    return typeof value === 'string' ? value.trim() : ''
  302  }

   25   * delegation tools (write/edit/delete/delegate_task/...) so the child can

   31    'grep',
   32    'glob',

   47    'Use this first for any repository or project exploration: locating files or symbols, searching code or keywords, tracing call paths or dependencies, understanding architecture or behavior, or gathering context before a change.',
   48    'Complex questions MUST be split into multiple parallel explore_agent calls with non-overlapping scopes (for example one call for API wiring, another for UI, another for tests). Never pack a whole-repo investigation into a single call.',
   49    'Each call needs a short distinct title (2-6 words) for the UI plus a narrow, self-contained query that states what evidence to return.',
   50    '即使后续需要修改文件，也必须先调用 explore_agent；它优先于主代理直接使用 read/grep/glob/ls/repo_map/find/bash，并应为独立调查面并行发起多个调用。',

   55  /**
   56   * First-class `explore_agent` tool: the main agent delegates a scoped
   57   * exploration query to a read-oriented child that may use full bash plus the
   58   * exploration allow-list. It reuses the whole subagent runtime (child thread,
   59   * events, approval inheritance, SubagentCallCard rendering) while keeping the
   60   * delegate_task router untouched. Lab disable is enforced live via
   61   * `shouldAdvertise` (and an execute backstop) so hot-applied settings can
   62   * hide or restore the tool without rebuilding the provider away.
   63   */
   64  export function buildExploreAgentToolProvider(
   65    runtime: DelegationRuntime | undefined,
   66    config: () => ExploreAgentToolConfig | undefined
   67  ): CapabilityToolProvider[] {
   68    if (!runtime?.enabled()) return []
   69    const shouldAdvertise = (_context: ToolHostContext): boolean =>
   70      config()?.enabled !== false

extension_tool_call

模型可调用的内置工具 · tool registry · kun/src/adapters/tool/extension-tool-provider.ts:486
源码 ↗

Call one tool from the current thread\

extension_tool_call→解析→路由→策略→处理器→回执
展开逐行源码证据
  481              const limit = typeof args.limit === 'number' ? args.limit : 5
  482              return { output: { epochId: epoch.id, tools: this.searchCatalogEpoch(epoch, query, limit) } }
  483            }
  484          }),
  485          LocalToolHost.defineTool({
  486            name: 'extension_tool_call',
  487            description: 'Call one tool from the current thread\'s pinned extension-tool catalog.',
  488            policy: 'auto',
  489            inputSchema: {
  490              type: 'object',
  491              properties: {
  492                canonicalToolId: { type: 'string', minLength: 1 },
  493                arguments: { type: 'object' }
  494              },
  495              required: ['canonicalToolId', 'arguments'],
  496              additionalProperties: false
  497            },
  498            shouldAdvertise: (context) => this.shouldAdvertiseGateway(context),
  499            execute: async (args, context, onUpdate) => {
  500              const epoch = requiredEpoch(context)
  501              const canonicalToolId = typeof args.canonicalToolId === 'string' ? args.canonicalToolId : ''
  502              const toolArgs = isPlainObject(args.arguments) ? args.arguments : {}
  503              if (!epoch.canonicalToolIds.includes(canonicalToolId)) {
  504                return { output: { code: 'extension_tool_not_pinned', error: 'Tool is not in the active catalog epoch.' }, isError: true }
  505              }
  506              this.assertCatalogCurrent(epoch)
  507              const registration = this.registrationForWorkspace(canonicalToolId, context.workspace)
  508              if (!registration) throw new Error(`pinned extension tool is unavailable: ${canonicalToolId}`)
  509              const targetCallId = `gateway_${stableHash({
  510                threadId: context.threadId,
  511                turnId: context.turnId,
  512                canonicalToolId,
  513                arguments: toolArgs
  514              }).slice(0, 20)}`
  515              const result = await this.gatewayDispatchHost.execute({
  516                callId: targetCallId,
  517                toolName: registration.modelAlias,
  518                providerId: extensionProviderId(registration.principal.extensionId),
  519                toolKind: registration.declaration.sideEffect === 'workspace-write' ? 'file_change' : 'tool_call',
  520                arguments: toolArgs
  521              }, {
  522                ...context,
  523                // The gateway already verified the persisted epoch and target.
  524                // Clear it for the nested dispatch so the target's direct-model
  525                // advertisement threshold does not block broker execution.
  526                extensionToolCatalogEpoch: undefined,
  527                allowedToolNames: context.allowedToolNames
  528                  ? [...new Set([...context.allowedToolNames, registration.modelAlias])]
  529                  : undefined,
  530                allowedProviderIds: context.allowedProviderIds
  531                  ? [...new Set([...context.allowedProviderIds, extensionProviderId(registration.principal.extensionId)])]
  532                  : undefined
  533              }, async (item) => {
  534                if (item.kind === 'tool_result') await onUpdate?.({ output: item.output, isError: item.isError })
  535              })
  536              if (result.item.kind !== 'tool_result') {
  537                return { output: { code: 'extension_tool_invalid_result', error: 'Extension tool returned invalid history.' }, isError: true }
  538              }
  539              return {
  540                output: {
  541                  canonicalToolId,
  542                  sideEffect: registration.declaration.sideEffect,
  543                  result: result.item.output
  544                },
  545                ...(result.item.isError ? { isError: true } : {})
打开这条指令的动态执行图 →

fetch

模型可调用的内置工具 · tool registry · kun/src/adapters/tool/web-tool-provider.ts:249
源码 ↗

private readonly config: WebCapabilityConfig

fetch→解析→路由→策略→处理器→回执
展开逐行源码证据
  244      }
  245    })
  246  }
  247
  248  export class FetchWebProvider implements WebProvider {
  249    readonly id = 'fetch'
  250    private readonly config: WebCapabilityConfig
  251    private readonly nowIso: () => string
  252    private readonly resolveHost: (hostname: string) => Promise<ResolvedAddress[]>
  253    private readonly request: (request: FetchWebTransportRequest) => Promise<FetchWebTransportResponse>
  254
  255    constructor(config: WebCapabilityConfig, options: FetchWebProviderOptions = {}) {
  256      this.config = config
  257      this.nowIso = options.nowIso ?? (() => new Date().toISOString())
  258      this.resolveHost = options.resolveHost ?? resolveHostAddresses
  259      this.request = options.request ?? requestWithPinnedLookup
  260    }
  261
  262    async fetch(request: {
  263      url: string
  264      maxBytes: number
  265      timeoutMs: number
  266      signal: AbortSignal
  267    }): Promise<WebFetchResult> {
  268      const controller = new AbortController()
  269      const timeout = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), request.timeoutMs)
  270      const onAbort = () => controller.abort()
  271      request.signal.addEventListener('abort', onAbort, { once: true })
  272      try {
  273        let currentUrl = new URL(request.url)
  274        let redirectCount = 0
  275        let response: FetchWebTransportResponse | undefined
  276
  277        while (true) {
  278          const policy = validateUrlPolicy(currentUrl.href, this.config)
  279          if (!policy.ok) throw new WebFetchPolicyError(policy.reason)
  280          const resolved = await awaitWithAbort(this.resolveDestination(policy.url), controller.signal)
  281          response = await this.request({
  282            url: policy.url,
  283            lookup: pinnedLookup(policy.url.hostname, resolved),
  284            signal: controller.signal
  285          })
  286
  287          if (!isRedirectStatus(response.status)) break
  288          const location = response.location
  289          response.cancel()
  290          if (!location) throw new Error(`HTTP ${response.status} redirect is missing a Location header`)
  291          if (redirectCount >= MAX_WEB_REDIRECTS) throw new Error(`redirect limit (${MAX_WEB_REDIRECTS}) exceeded`)
  292          try {
  293            currentUrl = new URL(location, policy.url)
  294          } catch {
  295            throw new WebFetchPolicyError('redirect Location must be a valid absolute or relative HTTP URL')
  296          }
  297          redirectCount += 1
  298        }
  299
  300        if (!response) throw new Error('web response is unavailable')
  301        if (response.status < 200 || response.status >= 300) {
  302          response.cancel()
  303          throw new Error(`HTTP ${response.status}`)
  304        }
  305
  306        // Oversized pages truncate at maxBytes via the streaming read below.
  307        // Hard-failing on the declared content-length made most real pages
  308        // unfetchable whenever the model passed a small byte budget.
打开这条指令的动态执行图 →

  117            pattern,

  144            absolutePath

  256
  257  export function createGrepLocalTool(options: GrepLocalToolOptions = {}): LocalTool {
  258    return LocalToolHost.defineTool({
  259      name: 'grep',
  260      description: 'Search file contents for a pattern and return matching lines with paths and line numbers.',
  261      inputSchema: {
  262        type: 'object',
  263        properties: {
  264          pattern: { type: 'string' },
  265          path: { type: 'string' },
  266          glob: { type: 'string' },
  267          ignoreCase: { type: 'boolean' },
  268          literal: { type: 'boolean' },
  269          context: { type: 'number' },
  270          limit: { type: 'number' },
  271          cursor: { type: 'string', description: 'Opaque cursor from a previous identical grep query.' }
  272        },
  273        required: ['pattern'],
  274        additionalProperties: false
  275      },
  276      policy: 'auto',
  277      execute: async (args, context) => withToolBoundary(async () => {
  278        const pattern = typeof args.pattern === 'string' ? args.pattern : ''
  279        if (!pattern.trim()) return { output: { error: 'pattern is required' }, isError: true }
  280        const literal = normalizeBoolean(args.literal)
  281        const ignoreCase = normalizeBoolean(args.ignoreCase)
  282        const contextLines = typeof args.context === 'number' && Number.isFinite(args.context) && args.context > 0
  283          ? Math.min(DEFAULT_GREP_MAX_CONTEXT_LINES, Math.floor(args.context))
  284          : 0
  285        const glob = typeof args.glob === 'string' && args.glob.trim() ? args.glob.trim() : null
  286        const limit = normalizePositiveInteger(args.limit, options.defaultLimit ?? defaultSourcePageLimit(context))
  287        const maxFileBytes = options.maxFileBytes
  288        const maxTotalBytes = options.maxTotalBytes
  289        const rawPath = typeof args.path === 'string' && args.path.trim() ? args.path : '.'
  290        const query = JSON.stringify({ pattern, rawPath, glob, ignoreCase, literal, context: contextLines })
  291        const cursor = parseCursor(args.cursor, query)
  292        if (cursor instanceof Error) return { output: { code: 'invalid_cursor', error: cursor.message }, isError: true }
  293        const scanLimit = sourceScanLimit(cursor, limit)
  294        const flags = ignoreCase ? 'i' : ''
  295        const effectiveMatcher = literal
  296          ? new RegExp(pattern.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, '\\$&'), flags)
  297          : new RegExp(pattern, flags)
  298        const globMatcher = glob ? globToRegExp(glob.includes('/') ? glob : `**/${glob}`) : null
  299        const { workspaceRoot: root, absolutePath, relativePath } = await resolveWorkspacePath(rawPath, context)
  300        if (options.operations?.search) {
  301          const matches = await options.operations.search({
  302            pattern,
  303            path: absolutePath,
  304            glob,
  305            ignoreCase,
  306            literal,
  307            context: contextLines,
  308            limit: scanLimit
  309          })
  310          return {
  311            output: {
  312              path: absolutePath,
  313              relative_path: relativePath,
  314              pattern,
  315              glob,
  316              ignore_case: ignoreCase,
  317              literal,
  318              context: contextLines,

grok-imagine-image

private readonly endpointUrl: string

grok-imagine-image→解析→路由→策略→处理器→回执
展开逐行源码证据
 1046        : 'Volcano Ark image provider returned no image data')
 1047    }
 1048  }
 1049
 1050  export class GrokImagineImageClient implements ImageGenClient {
 1051    readonly id = 'grok-imagine-image'
 1052    private readonly endpointUrl: string
 1053
 1054    constructor(
 1055      baseUrl: string,
 1056      private readonly apiKey: string,
 1057      private readonly headers: Record<string, string> = {}
 1058    ) {
 1059      this.endpointUrl = openAiCompatImageUrl(baseUrl, 'generations')
 1060    }
 1061
 1062    async generate(request: ImageGenRequest): Promise<GeneratedImage> {
 1063      const signal = withTimeout(request.signal, request.timeoutMs)
 1064      let response: Response
 1065      try {
 1066        response = await fetch(this.endpointUrl, {
 1067          method: 'POST',
 1068          headers: {
 1069            ...this.headers,
 1070            Authorization: `Bearer ${this.apiKey}`,
 1071            'Content-Type': 'application/json'
 1072          },
 1073          body: JSON.stringify({
 1074            model: request.model,
 1075            prompt: request.prompt,
 1076            n: 1,
 1077            aspect_ratio: request.aspectRatio ?? 'auto',
 1078            resolution: grokImagineResolution(request.size),
 1079            response_format: 'b64_json'
 1080          }),
 1081          signal
 1082        })
 1083      } catch (error) {
 1084        throw imageFetchFailure(this.endpointUrl, error, request)
 1085      }
 1086      const text = await response.text()
 1087      if (!response.ok) throw new ImageGenHttpError(response.status, text)
 1088      let payload: ImagesApiPayload
 1089      try {
 1090        payload = JSON.parse(text) as ImagesApiPayload
 1091      } catch {
 1092        throw new Error('Grok Imagine image provider returned invalid JSON')
 1093      }
 1094      const b64 = payload.data?.[0]?.b64_json
 1095      if (!b64) throw new Error('Grok Imagine image provider returned no image data')
 1096      return { data: Buffer.from(b64, 'base64'), mimeType: 'image/jpeg' }
 1097    }
 1098
 1099    async edit(_request: ImageGenEditRequest): Promise<GeneratedImage> {
 1100      throw new Error('Grok Imagine image editing is not supported by this tool')
 1101    }
 1102  }
 1103
 1104  function grokImagineResolution(size: string | undefined): '1k' | '2k' {
 1105    const longEdge = size ? parseSizeLongEdge(size) : undefined
 1106    return longEdge && longEdge >= SIZE_TIERS['2K'] ? '2k' : '1k'
 1107  }

展开逐行源码证据
  878      }
  879    }
  880  }
  881
  882  export class GrokImagineVideoClient implements VideoGenClient {
  883    readonly id = 'grok-imagine-video'
  884    private readonly rootUrl: string
  885
  886    constructor(
  887      baseUrl: string,
  888      private readonly apiKey: string,
  889      private readonly extraHeaders: Record<string, string> = {}
  890    ) {
  891      this.rootUrl = trimTrailingSlashes(baseUrl)
  892    }
  893
  894    async generate(request: VideoGenRequest): Promise<GeneratedMedia> {
  895      if (request.lastFrameImage) {
  896        throw new Error('Grok Imagine video does not support an explicit last frame')
  897      }
  898      const signal = withTimeout(request.signal, request.timeoutMs)
  899      const createPayload = await requestJson<GrokVideoCreatePayload>(`${this.rootUrl}/videos/generations`, {
  900        method: 'POST',
  901        headers: this.headers(),
  902        body: JSON.stringify({
  903          model: request.model,
  904          prompt: request.prompt,
  905          duration: request.duration,
  906          resolution: request.resolution.toLowerCase(),
  907          ...(request.firstFrameImage
  908            ? { image: { url: dataUri(request.firstFrameImage.mimeType, request.firstFrameImage.data) } }
  909            : request.aspectRatio
  910              ? { aspect_ratio: request.aspectRatio }
  911              : {}),
  912          reference_images: []
  913        }),
  914        signal
  915      }, request)
  916      const requestId = createPayload.request_id?.trim()
  917      if (!requestId) throw new Error('Grok Imagine video provider returned no request_id')
  918      await request.onUpdate?.({
  919        output: { status: 'submitted', taskId: requestId, provider: this.id }
  920      })
  921
  922      const deadline = Date.now() + request.timeoutMs
  923      let lastStatus = 'submitted'
  924      while (Date.now() < deadline) {
  925        await delay(request.pollIntervalMs, signal)
  926        const pollPayload = await requestJson<GrokVideoPollPayload>(
  927          `${this.rootUrl}/videos/${encodeURIComponent(requestId)}`,
  928          { method: 'GET', headers: this.headers(), signal },
  929          request
  930        )
  931        lastStatus = pollPayload.status?.trim().toLowerCase() || lastStatus
  932        await request.onUpdate?.({
  933          output: { status: lastStatus, taskId: requestId, provider: this.id }
  934        })
  935        if (lastStatus === 'failed' || lastStatus === 'expired') {
  936          throw new Error(`Grok Imagine video generation ${lastStatus} (request_id=${requestId})`)
  937        }
  938        if (lastStatus !== 'done') continue
  939        const downloadUrl = pollPayload.video?.url?.trim()
  940        if (!downloadUrl) throw new Error('Grok Imagine video provider finished without a download URL')
  941        const response = await requestResponse(downloadUrl, { method: 'GET', signal }, request)
  942        if (!response.ok) throw new ImageGenHttpError(response.status, await response.text())

  158  /**
  159   * Map UI-friendly aspect ratio + size tier to an OpenAI-compatible "WxH"
  160   * size string. Long edge anchors to the explicit tier first, then a custom
  161   * default size, then the configured default resolution. Short edge follows the
  162   * ratio snapped to multiples of 64 with a 256px floor. `auto` is passed through
  163   * when no ratio is requested; a ratio needs concrete dimensions, so an `auto`
  164   * resolution uses a compatible 1K fallback.
  165   */
  166  export function mapImageSize(
  167    aspectRatio: string | undefined,
  168    imageSize: string | undefined,
  169    defaultSize: string | undefined,
  170    defaultResolution: ImageGenerationResolution = '1K'
  171  ): string | undefined {
  172    if (imageSize) {
  173      return sizeForLongEdge(aspectRatio, SIZE_TIERS[imageSize] ?? COMPATIBLE_SIZE_FALLBACK)
  174    }
  175
  176    if (defaultSize) {
  177      if (!aspectRatio) return defaultSize
  178      const longEdge = parseSizeLongEdge(defaultSize)
  179      if (longEdge) return sizeForLongEdge(aspectRatio, longEdge)
  180    }
  181
  182    if (!aspectRatio && defaultResolution === 'auto') return 'auto'
  183    return sizeForLongEdge(
  184      aspectRatio,
  185      SIZE_TIERS[defaultResolution] ?? COMPATIBLE_SIZE_FALLBACK
  186    )
  187  }
  188
  189  function sizeForLongEdge(aspectRatio: string | undefined, longEdge: number): string {
  190    const parsed = parseRatio(aspectRatio)
  191    if (!parsed) return `${longEdge}x${longEdge}`
  192    const { w, h } = parsed
打开这条指令的动态执行图 →

  186      }
  187      // Evaluate before Promise.resolve so existing callers retain synchronous
  188      // catalog-drift validation when no lazy preparation is configured.
  189      return Promise.resolve(components.registry.listTools(context))
  190    }

  194    }

   27
   28  export function createLsLocalTool(options: LsLocalToolOptions = {}): LocalTool {
   29    const statOp = options.operations?.stat ?? defaultLsLocalToolOperations.stat!
   30    const readdirOp = options.operations?.readdir ?? defaultLsLocalToolOperations.readdir!
   31    return LocalToolHost.defineTool({
   32      name: 'ls',
   33      description: 'List directory contents. Returns entries sorted alphabetically and marks directories.',
   34      inputSchema: {
   35        type: 'object',
   36        properties: {
   37          path: { type: 'string' },
   38          limit: { type: 'number' }
   39        },
   40        required: [],
   41        additionalProperties: false
   42      },
   43      policy: 'auto',
   44      execute: async (args, context) => withToolBoundary(async () => {
   45        const rawPath = typeof args.path === 'string' && args.path.trim() ? args.path : '.'
   46        const limit = normalizePositiveInteger(args.limit, options.defaultLimit ?? DEFAULT_LIST_LIMIT)
   47        const { workspaceRoot: root, absolutePath, relativePath } = await resolveWorkspacePath(rawPath, context)
   48        const targetStat = await statOp(absolutePath)
   49        if (!targetStat.isDirectory()) {
   50          return {
   51            output: {
   52              error: `not a directory: ${absolutePath}`,
   53              path: absolutePath
   54            },
   55            isError: true
   56          }
   57        }
   58        const entries = await listDirectoryWithOps(absolutePath, root, false, limit, statOp, readdirOp)
   59        return {
   60          output: {
   61            path: absolutePath,
   62            relative_path: relativePath,
   63            entries: entries.map((entry) => ({
   64              ...entry,
   65              display_name: entry.kind === 'directory' ? `${entry.name}/` : entry.name
   66            })),
   67            names: entries.map((entry) => (entry.kind === 'directory' ? `${entry.name}/` : entry.name)),
   68            truncated: entries.length >= limit,
   69            entry_limit_reached: entries.length >= limit ? limit : null
   70          }
   71        }
   72      })
   73    })
   74  }

mcp_get_prompt

模型可调用的内置工具 · tool registry · kun/src/adapters/tool/mcp-facade-provider.ts:157
源码 ↗

Get one MCP prompt from a connected MCP server.

mcp_get_prompt→解析→路由→策略→处理器→回执
展开逐行源码证据
  152    })
  153  }
  154
  155  function createGetPromptTool(connected: McpFacadeConnectionState[]): LocalTool {
  156    return LocalToolHost.defineTool({
  157      name: 'mcp_get_prompt',
  158      description: 'Get one MCP prompt from a connected MCP server.',
  159      policy: 'on-request',
  160      sideEffect: 'read-only',
  161      toolKind: 'command_execution',
  162      inputSchema: {
  163        type: 'object',
  164        properties: {
  165          serverId: { type: 'string' },
  166          name: { type: 'string' },
  167          arguments: { type: 'object', additionalProperties: true }
  168        },
  169        required: ['name']
  170      },
  171      shouldAdvertise: (context) => hasUsableServer(connected, context, 'getPrompt'),
  172      execute: async (args, context) => {
  173        const name = stringArg(args.name)
  174        if (!name) return { output: { error: 'name is required' }, isError: true }
  175        const state = selectSingleUsableServer(connected, context, 'getPrompt', stringArg(args.serverId))
  176        if (!state) return unavailableOutput('getPrompt')
  177        const result = await state.client.getPrompt?.(
  178          { name, arguments: objectArg(args.arguments) },
  179          { signal: context.abortSignal, timeout: state.server.timeoutMs }
  180        )
  181        return { output: { serverId: state.serverId, name, result } }
  182      }
  183    })
  184  }
  185
  186  function hasUsableServer(
  187    connected: McpFacadeConnectionState[],
  188    context: ToolHostContext,
  189    capability: FacadeCapability
  190  ): boolean {
  191    return connected.some((state) => isUsableServer(state, context, capability))
  192  }
  193
  194  function selectUsableServers(
  195    connected: McpFacadeConnectionState[],
  196    context: ToolHostContext,
  197    capability: FacadeCapability,
  198    serverId?: string
  199  ): McpFacadeConnectionState[] {
  200    return connected.filter((state) => {
  201      if (serverId && state.serverId !== serverId) return false
  202      return isUsableServer(state, context, capability)
  203    })
  204  }
  205
  206  function selectSingleUsableServer(
  207    connected: McpFacadeConnectionState[],
  208    context: ToolHostContext,
  209    capability: FacadeCapability,
  210    serverId?: string
  211  ): McpFacadeConnectionState | null {
  212    const states = selectUsableServers(connected, context, capability, serverId)
  213    return states[0] ?? null
  214  }
  215
  216  function isUsableServer(
打开这条指令的动态执行图 →

mcp_list_prompts

模型可调用的内置工具 · tool registry · kun/src/adapters/tool/mcp-facade-provider.ts:130
源码 ↗

List MCP prompts exposed by currently connected MCP servers.

mcp_list_prompts→解析→路由→策略→处理器→回执
展开逐行源码证据
  125    })
  126  }
  127
  128  function createListPromptsTool(connected: McpFacadeConnectionState[]): LocalTool {
  129    return LocalToolHost.defineTool({
  130      name: 'mcp_list_prompts',
  131      description: 'List MCP prompts exposed by currently connected MCP servers.',
  132      policy: 'on-request',
  133      sideEffect: 'read-only',
  134      toolKind: 'command_execution',
  135      inputSchema: {
  136        type: 'object',
  137        properties: {
  138          serverId: { type: 'string' }
  139        }
  140      },
  141      shouldAdvertise: (context) => hasUsableServer(connected, context, 'listPrompts'),
  142      execute: async (args, context) => {
  143        const states = selectUsableServers(connected, context, 'listPrompts', stringArg(args.serverId))
  144        if (!states.length) return unavailableOutput('listPrompts')
  145        const results = []
  146        for (const state of states) {
  147          const listed = await state.client.listPrompts?.({ signal: context.abortSignal, timeout: state.server.timeoutMs })
  148          results.push({ serverId: state.serverId, prompts: listed?.prompts ?? [], nextCursor: listed?.nextCursor })
  149        }
  150        return { output: { servers: results } }
  151      }
  152    })
  153  }
  154
  155  function createGetPromptTool(connected: McpFacadeConnectionState[]): LocalTool {
  156    return LocalToolHost.defineTool({
  157      name: 'mcp_get_prompt',
  158      description: 'Get one MCP prompt from a connected MCP server.',
  159      policy: 'on-request',
  160      sideEffect: 'read-only',
  161      toolKind: 'command_execution',
  162      inputSchema: {
  163        type: 'object',
  164        properties: {
  165          serverId: { type: 'string' },
  166          name: { type: 'string' },
  167          arguments: { type: 'object', additionalProperties: true }
  168        },
  169        required: ['name']
  170      },
  171      shouldAdvertise: (context) => hasUsableServer(connected, context, 'getPrompt'),
  172      execute: async (args, context) => {
  173        const name = stringArg(args.name)
  174        if (!name) return { output: { error: 'name is required' }, isError: true }
  175        const state = selectSingleUsableServer(connected, context, 'getPrompt', stringArg(args.serverId))
  176        if (!state) return unavailableOutput('getPrompt')
  177        const result = await state.client.getPrompt?.(
  178          { name, arguments: objectArg(args.arguments) },
  179          { signal: context.abortSignal, timeout: state.server.timeoutMs }
  180        )
  181        return { output: { serverId: state.serverId, name, result } }
  182      }
  183    })

   41    }

   44  function createListResourcesTool(connected: McpFacadeConnectionState[]): LocalTool {
   45    return LocalToolHost.defineTool({
   46      name: 'mcp_list_resources',
   47      description: 'List MCP resources exposed by currently connected MCP servers.',
   48      // Remote MCP servers are an external authority. Listing can expose
   49      // sensitive metadata, so facade RPCs use the same approval boundary as
   50      // direct MCP calls rather than treating server annotations as authority.
   51      policy: 'on-request',
   52      sideEffect: 'read-only',
   53      toolKind: 'command_execution',
   54      inputSchema: {
   55        type: 'object',
   56        properties: {
   57          serverId: { type: 'string' }
   58        }
   59      },
   60      shouldAdvertise: (context) => hasUsableServer(connected, context, 'listResources'),
   61      execute: async (args, context) => {
   62        const states = selectUsableServers(connected, context, 'listResources', stringArg(args.serverId))
   63        if (!states.length) return unavailableOutput('listResources')
   64        const results = []
   65        for (const state of states) {
   66          const listed = await state.client.listResources?.({ signal: context.abortSignal, timeout: state.server.timeoutMs })
   67          results.push({ serverId: state.serverId, resources: listed?.resources ?? [], nextCursor: listed?.nextCursor })
   68        }
   69        return { output: { servers: results } }
   70      }
   71    })
   72  }
   73
   74  function createReadResourceTool(connected: McpFacadeConnectionState[]): LocalTool {
   75    return LocalToolHost.defineTool({
   76      name: 'mcp_read_resource',
   77      description: 'Read one MCP resource from a connected MCP server.',
   78      policy: 'on-request',
   79      sideEffect: 'read-only',
   80      toolKind: 'command_execution',
   81      inputSchema: {
   82        type: 'object',
   83        properties: {
   84          serverId: { type: 'string' },
   85          uri: { type: 'string' }
   86        },
   87        required: ['uri']
   88      },
   89      shouldAdvertise: (context) => hasUsableServer(connected, context, 'readResource'),
   90      execute: async (args, context) => {
   91        const uri = stringArg(args.uri)
   92        if (!uri) return { output: { error: 'uri is required' }, isError: true }
   93        const state = selectSingleUsableServer(connected, context, 'readResource', stringArg(args.serverId))
   94        if (!state) return unavailableOutput('readResource')
   95        const result = await state.client.readResource?.({ uri }, { signal: context.abortSignal, timeout: state.server.timeoutMs })
   96        return { output: { serverId: state.serverId, uri, result } }
   97      }
   98    })
   99  }

  179  export function mcpSearchDiagnostic(input: {
  180    config: McpSearchConfig
  181    active: boolean
  182    indexedToolCount: number
  183    advertisedToolCount: number
  184    state: McpSearchCatalogState
  185  }): McpSearchRuntimeDiagnostic {
  186    return {
  187      enabled: input.config.enabled,
  188      mode: input.config.mode,
  189      active: input.active,
  190      indexedToolCount: input.indexedToolCount,
  191      advertisedToolCount: input.advertisedToolCount,
  192      topKDefault: input.config.topKDefault,
  193      topKMax: input.config.topKMax,
  194      minScore: input.config.minScore,
  195      ...(input.state.lastRefreshedAt ? { lastRefreshedAt: input.state.lastRefreshedAt } : {}),
  196      ...(input.state.lastError ? { lastError: input.state.lastError } : {}),
  197      ...(input.state.catalogFingerprint ? { catalogFingerprint: input.state.catalogFingerprint } : {}),
  198      ...(input.state.catalogDrift !== undefined ? { catalogDrift: input.state.catalogDrift } : {})
  199    }
  200  }
  201
  202  export function tokenizeMcpSearchText(text = ''): string[] {
  203    const source = normalizeLower(text)
  204    const tokens: string[] = []
  205
  206    const latinTerms = source.match(/[a-z0-9][a-z0-9_-]{1,}/g) ?? []
  207    for (const term of latinTerms) {
  208      for (const part of term.split(/[_-]+/)) {
  209        if (tokenAllowed(part)) tokens.push(part)
  210      }
  211      if (tokenAllowed(term)) tokens.push(term)
  212    }
  213
  214    const hanSegments = source.match(/\p{Script=Han}+/gu) ?? []
  215    for (const segment of hanSegments) {
  216      const chars = [...segment].slice(0, 80)
  217      if (chars.length === 1) {
  218        tokens.push(chars[0])
  219        continue
  220      }
  221      for (let size = 2; size <= Math.min(4, chars.length); size += 1) {
  222        for (let index = 0; index <= chars.length - size; index += 1) {
  223          tokens.push(chars.slice(index, index + size).join(''))
  224        }
  225      }
  226    }
  227
  228    return tokens
  229  }

  585  export class MimoSpeechClient implements SpeechGenClient {
  586    readonly id = 'mimo-tts'
  587    private readonly endpointUrl: string
  588
  589    constructor(
  590      baseUrl: string,
  591      private readonly apiKey: string
  592    ) {
  593      this.endpointUrl = apiUrl(baseUrl, '/v1/chat/completions')
  594    }
  595
  596    async generate(request: SpeechGenRequest): Promise<GeneratedMedia> {
  597      const messages = [
  598        ...(request.style ? [{ role: 'user', content: request.style }] : []),
  599        { role: 'assistant', content: request.text }
  600      ]
  601      const payload = await requestJson<MimoSpeechPayload>(this.endpointUrl, {
  602        method: 'POST',
  603        headers: {
  604          Authorization: `Bearer ${this.apiKey}`,
  605          'api-key': this.apiKey,
  606          'Content-Type': 'application/json'
  607        },
  608        body: JSON.stringify({
  609          model: request.model,
  610          messages,
  611          audio: {
  612            format: request.format,
  613            ...(request.voice ? { voice: request.voice } : {})
  614          }
  615        }),
  616        signal: withTimeout(request.signal, request.timeoutMs)
  617      }, request)
  618      const audio = payload.choices?.[0]?.message?.audio?.data
  619      if (!audio) throw new Error('MiMo speech provider returned no audio data')
  620      return {
  621        data: Buffer.from(audio, 'base64'),
  622        mimeType: audioMimeType(request.format),
  623        extension: audioExtension(request.format)
  624      }
  625    }
  626  }

  628  export class MiniMaxMusicClient implements MusicGenClient {
  629    readonly id = 'minimax-music'
  630    private readonly endpointUrl: string
  631
  632    constructor(
  633      baseUrl: string,
  634      private readonly apiKey: string
  635    ) {
  636      this.endpointUrl = apiUrl(baseUrl, '/v1/music_generation')
  637    }
  638
  639    async generate(request: MusicGenRequest): Promise<GeneratedMedia> {
  640      const payload = await requestJson<MiniMaxAudioPayload>(this.endpointUrl, {
  641        method: 'POST',
  642        headers: {
  643          Authorization: `Bearer ${this.apiKey}`,
  644          'Content-Type': 'application/json'
  645        },
  646        body: JSON.stringify({
  647          model: request.model,
  648          ...(request.prompt ? { prompt: request.prompt } : {}),
  649          ...(request.lyrics ? { lyrics: request.lyrics } : {}),
  650          output_format: 'hex',
  651          audio_setting: {
  652            format: request.format,
  653            sample_rate: 44_100,
  654            bitrate: 256_000
  655          },
  656          lyrics_optimizer: request.lyricsOptimizer ?? (!request.lyrics && request.instrumental !== true),
  657          ...(request.instrumental !== undefined ? { is_instrumental: request.instrumental } : {}),
  658          ...(request.referenceAudioUrl ? { audio_url: request.referenceAudioUrl } : {})
  659        }),
  660        signal: withTimeout(request.signal, request.timeoutMs)
  661      }, request)
  662      assertMiniMaxOk(payload.base_resp, 'MiniMax music provider')
  663      const audio = payload.data?.audio
  664      if (!audio) throw new Error('MiniMax music provider returned no audio data')
  665      return {
  666        data: bufferFromHex(audio),
  667        mimeType: audioMimeType(request.format),
  668        extension: audioExtension(request.format)
  669      }
  670    }
  671  }

  547    async generate(request: SpeechGenRequest): Promise<GeneratedMedia> {
  548      const voiceId = request.voice || 'male-qn-qingse'
  549      const payload = await requestJson<MiniMaxAudioPayload>(this.endpointUrl, {
  550        method: 'POST',
  551        headers: {
  552          Authorization: `Bearer ${this.apiKey}`,
  553          'Content-Type': 'application/json'
  554        },
  555        body: JSON.stringify({
  556          model: request.model,
  557          text: request.text,
  558          output_format: 'hex',
  559          voice_setting: {
  560            voice_id: voiceId,
  561            speed: 1,
  562            vol: 1,
  563            pitch: 0
  564          },
  565          audio_setting: {
  566            format: request.format,
  567            sample_rate: request.format === 'mp3' ? 32_000 : 44_100,
  568            bitrate: 128_000,
  569            channel: 1
  570          }
  571        }),
  572        signal: withTimeout(request.signal, request.timeoutMs)
  573      }, request)
  574      assertMiniMaxOk(payload.base_resp, 'MiniMax speech provider')
  575      const audio = payload.data?.audio
  576      if (!audio) throw new Error('MiniMax speech provider returned no audio data')
  577      return {
  578        data: bufferFromHex(audio),
  579        mimeType: audioMimeType(request.format),
  580        extension: audioExtension(request.format)
  581      }
  582    }
  583  }

  673  export class MiniMaxVideoClient implements VideoGenClient {
  674    readonly id = 'minimax-video'
  675    private readonly rootUrl: string
  676
  677    constructor(
  678      baseUrl: string,
  679      private readonly apiKey: string
  680    ) {
  681      this.rootUrl = minimaxRootUrl(baseUrl)
  682    }
  683
  684    async generate(request: VideoGenRequest): Promise<GeneratedMedia> {
  685      const signal = withTimeout(request.signal, request.timeoutMs)
  686      const createPayload = await requestJson<MiniMaxVideoCreatePayload>(`${this.rootUrl}/v1/video_generation`, {
  687        method: 'POST',
  688        headers: this.headers(),
  689        body: JSON.stringify({
  690          model: request.model,
  691          prompt: request.prompt,
  692          duration: request.duration,
  693          resolution: request.resolution,
  694          ...(request.firstFrameImage
  695            ? { first_frame_image: dataUri(request.firstFrameImage.mimeType, request.firstFrameImage.data) }
  696            : {}),
  697          ...(request.lastFrameImage
  698            ? { last_frame_image: dataUri(request.lastFrameImage.mimeType, request.lastFrameImage.data) }
  699            : {})
  700        }),
  701        signal
  702      }, request)
  703      assertMiniMaxOk(createPayload.base_resp, 'MiniMax video provider')
  704      const taskId = createPayload.task_id
  705      if (!taskId) throw new Error('MiniMax video provider returned no task_id')
  706      await request.onUpdate?.({
  707        output: { status: 'submitted', taskId, provider: this.id }
  708      })
  709
  710      const deadline = Date.now() + request.timeoutMs
  711      let lastStatus = 'submitted'
  712      while (Date.now() < deadline) {
  713        await delay(request.pollIntervalMs, signal)
  714        const queryUrl = new URL(`${this.rootUrl}/v1/query/video_generation`)
  715        queryUrl.searchParams.set('task_id', taskId)
  716        const queryPayload = await requestJson<MiniMaxVideoQueryPayload>(queryUrl.toString(), {
  717          method: 'GET',
  718          headers: this.headers(),
  719          signal
  720        }, request)
  721        assertMiniMaxOk(queryPayload.base_resp, 'MiniMax video provider')
  722        lastStatus = queryPayload.status || lastStatus
  723        await request.onUpdate?.({
  724          output: { status: lastStatus, taskId, provider: this.id }
  725        })
  726        if (isFailureStatus(lastStatus)) {
  727          throw new Error(`MiniMax video generation failed with status ${lastStatus}`)
  728        }
  729        if (!isSuccessStatus(lastStatus)) continue
  730        const fileId = queryPayload.file_id
  731        if (!fileId) throw new Error('MiniMax video provider finished without file_id')
  732        const downloadUrl = await this.retrieveDownloadUrl(fileId, request)
  733        const response = await requestResponse(downloadUrl, { method: 'GET', signal }, request)

  102  function operationSummaries(ops: readonly unknown[]): RepairOperationSummary[] {
  103    return ops.map((op, index) => ({
  104      index,
  105      op: opName(op),
  106      targetIds: targetIdsForOp(op)
  107    }))
  108  }
  109
  110  function recommendations(options: BuildDesignRepairReportOptions): RepairRecommendation[] {
  111    if (options.errors.length > 0 || options.unresolvedFindings.length > 0) {
  112      return [{
  113        toolId: 'design.ops',
  114        reason: 'Some repair work could not be applied automatically.',
  115        inputHint: 'Pass explicit ShapeOp repairs for unresolved findings or failed operation targets.'
  116      }]
  117    }
  118    return [{
  119      toolId: 'design.critique',
  120      reason: 'Repair operations were applied; run critique again to verify the design state.',
  121      inputHint: 'Use the affectedIds from this repair result as scopeIds.'
  122    }]
  123  }
  124
  125  export function buildDesignRepairReport(options: BuildDesignRepairReportOptions): DesignRepairReport {
  126    const blocked = options.ops.length === 0 || (
  127      options.repairedFindings.length === 0 && options.findings.length > 0
  128    )
  129    const status = options.errors.length > 0 || options.unresolvedFindings.length > 0
  130      ? 'partial'
  131      : blocked
  132        ? 'blocked'
  133        : 'applied'
  134    return {
  135      status,
  136      mode: options.mode,
  137      opCount: options.ops.length,
  138      affectedCount: options.result.affectedIds.length,
  139      findingCount: options.findings.length,
  140      repairedFindingCount: options.repairedFindings.length,
  141      unresolvedFindingCount: options.unresolvedFindings.length,
  142      noteResolutionCount: options.noteResolutionCount,
  143      categories: repairCategories(options),
  144      operationSummaries: operationSummaries(options.ops),
  145      unresolvedFindings: options.unresolvedFindings.map((finding) => ({ ...finding })),
  146      recommendations: recommendations(options)
  147    }
  148  }

  259    return LocalToolHost.defineTool({
  260      name: 'office_inspect',
  261      description:
  262        'Inspect an existing DOCX, XLSX, or PPTX file. Use this before office_edit. ' +
  263        'Returns the resolved path, document format, current SHA-256, and bounded structured content.',
  264      inputSchema: {
  265        type: 'object',
  266        properties: {
  267          path: { type: 'string' },
  268          action: {
  269            type: 'string',
  270            enum: ['summary', 'text', 'outline', 'query', 'issues', 'validate']

  277      },
  278      policy: 'auto',
  279      toolKind: 'tool_call',
  280      sideEffect: 'read-only',
  281      execute: async (args, context) => withToolBoundary(async () => {
  282        const rawPath = stringArgument(args.path)
  283        const action = stringArgument(args.action)
  284        const target = stringArgument(args.target)
  285        if (!rawPath || !isInspectAction(action)) {
  286          return { output: { error: 'path and a supported action are required' }, isError: true }
  287        }
  288        const resolvedPath = await resolveWorkspacePath(rawPath, context)
  289        const format = officeFormat(resolvedPath.absolutePath)
  290        const sourceSha256 = await sha256File(resolvedPath.absolutePath, context.abortSignal)
  291        const command = inspectCommand(
  292          resolvedPath.absolutePath,
  293          action,
  294          target,
  295          integerArgument(args.maxLines, 1, 4000) ?? 1000
  296        )
  297        const result = await runner.run(command, context.abortSignal)
  298        assertOfficeCliSuccess(result, `Office ${action} failed`)
  299        return {
  300          output: {
  301            path: resolvedPath.absolutePath,
  302            relative_path: resolvedPath.relativePath,
  303            format,
  304            source_sha256: sourceSha256,
  305            action,
  306            result: parseOfficeCliOutput(result.stdout)
  307          }
  308        }
  309      })

  313  function createOfficePreviewTool(runner: Pick<OfficeCliRunner, 'run'>): LocalTool {

  317        'Generate a bounded self-contained HTML preview for an existing DOCX, XLSX, or PPTX. ' +
  318        'Optionally scope it to a page, slide, sheet, or cell range when OfficeCLI supports that format.',
  319      inputSchema: {

  351            'screenshot',

  353            outputPath
  354          ]
  355          if (page) screenshotCommand.push('--page', String(page))
  356          const scopedRange = sheet
  357            ? `${sheet}!${range || 'A1:Z200'}`
  358            : range
  359          if (scopedRange) screenshotCommand.push('--range', scopedRange)
  360          try {
  361            const result = await runner.run(screenshotCommand, context.abortSignal)
  362            assertOfficeCliSuccess(result, 'Office visual preview failed')
  363            const image = await readFile(outputPath)
  364            if (image.byteLength <= 0 || image.byteLength > OFFICECLI_MAX_PREVIEW_BYTES) {
  365              throw new Error(
  366                `Office visual preview exceeds ${OFFICECLI_MAX_PREVIEW_BYTES} bytes.`
  367              )
  368            }
  369            return {
  370              output: {
  371                kind: 'image',
  372                path: resolvedPath.absolutePath,
  373                relative_path: resolvedPath.relativePath,
  374                format,

  855    }

  858
  859  export class OpenAiCompatImageClient implements ImageGenClient {
  860    readonly id = 'openai-compat'
  861    private readonly baseUrl: string
  862
  863    constructor(
  864      baseUrl: string,
  865      private readonly apiKey: string
  866    ) {
  867      this.baseUrl = trimTrailingSlashes(baseUrl)
  868    }
  869
  870    async generate(request: ImageGenRequest): Promise<GeneratedImage> {
  871      const body = (includeResponseFormat: boolean, includeQuality: boolean) =>
  872        JSON.stringify({
  873          model: request.model,
  874          prompt: request.prompt,
  875          n: 1,
  876          ...(request.size ? { size: request.size } : {}),
  877          ...(includeQuality && request.quality && request.quality !== 'auto' ? { quality: request.quality } : {}),
  878          ...(includeResponseFormat ? { response_format: 'b64_json' } : {})
  879        })
  880      return this.requestImage(
  881        openAiCompatImageUrl(this.baseUrl, 'generations'),
  882        (includeResponseFormat, includeQuality) => ({
  883          headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${this.apiKey}`, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
  884          body: body(includeResponseFormat, includeQuality)
  885        }),
  886        request
  887      )
  888    }
  889
  890    async edit(request: ImageGenEditRequest): Promise<GeneratedImage> {
  891      const buildForm = (includeResponseFormat: boolean, includeQuality: boolean) => {
  892        const form = new FormData()
  893        form.set('model', request.model)
  894        form.set('prompt', request.prompt)
  895        if (request.size) form.set('size', request.size)
  896        if (includeQuality && request.quality && request.quality !== 'auto') form.set('quality', request.quality)
  897        if (includeResponseFormat) form.set('response_format', 'b64_json')
  898        const field = request.images.length > 1 ? 'image[]' : 'image'
  899        for (const image of request.images) {
  900          form.append(field, new Blob([new Uint8Array(image.data)], { type: image.mimeType }), image.name)
  901        }
  902        return form
  903      }
  904      return this.requestImage(
  905        openAiCompatImageUrl(this.baseUrl, 'edits'),
  906        (includeResponseFormat, includeQuality) => ({

  503

  507    ) {

  509    }
  510
  511    async generate(request: SpeechGenRequest): Promise<GeneratedMedia> {
  512      const response = await requestResponse(this.endpointUrl, {
  513        method: 'POST',
  514        headers: {
  515          Authorization: `Bearer ${this.apiKey}`,
  516          'Content-Type': 'application/json'
  517        },
  518        body: JSON.stringify({
  519          model: request.model,
  520          input: request.text,
  521          voice: request.voice || 'alloy',
  522          response_format: request.format
  523        }),
  524        signal: withTimeout(request.signal, request.timeoutMs)
  525      }, request)
  526      if (!response.ok) throw new ImageGenHttpError(response.status, await response.text())
  527      const mimeType = response.headers.get('content-type')?.split(';')[0] || audioMimeType(request.format)
  528      return {
  529        data: Buffer.from(await response.arrayBuffer()),
  530        mimeType,
  531        extension: audioExtension(request.format)
  532      }
  533    }
  534  }

read

模型可调用的内置工具 · tool registry · kun/src/adapters/tool/builtin-read-tool.ts:30

Read a workspace file. Returns the largest contiguous line range that fits the current context; use next_offset to continue.

read→解析→路由→策略→处理器→回执
展开逐行源码证据
   25    // by the model-visible dispatch budget instead.
   26    const maxFileBytes = options.maxFileBytes === undefined
   27      ? undefined
   28      : normalizePositiveInteger(options.maxFileBytes, options.maxFileBytes)
   29    return LocalToolHost.defineTool({
   30      name: 'read',
   31      description: 'Read a workspace file. Returns the largest contiguous line range that fits the current context; use next_offset to continue.',
   32      inputSchema: {
   33        type: 'object',
   34        properties: {
   35          path: { type: 'string' },
   36          offset: { type: 'number' },
   37          limit: { type: 'number', description: 'Optional maximum number of lines for this page.' },
   38          expected_revision: { type: 'string', description: 'Revision returned by a previous page; prevents combining changed file versions.' }
   39        },
   40        required: ['path'],
   41        additionalProperties: false
   42      },
   43      policy: 'auto',
   44      toolKind: 'tool_call',
   45      sideEffect: 'read-only',
   46      execute: async (args, context) => withToolBoundary(async () => {
   47        const rawPath = typeof args.path === 'string' ? args.path : ''
   48        if (!rawPath.trim()) {
   49          return {
   50            output: {
   51              code: 'missing_path',
   52              error: 'path is required',
   53              hint: 'Pass a workspace-relative file path to read. If the target file is unknown, use ls, glob, or grep first.',
   54              expected_argument: { path: 'relative/path/from/workspace' },
   55              examples: [
   56                { path: 'README.md' },
   57                { path: 'src/main.ts' }
   58              ]
   59            },
   60            isError: true
   61          }
   62        }
   63        const { absolutePath, relativePath } = await resolveWorkspacePath(rawPath, context)
   64        const fileStat = await statOp(absolutePath)
   65        const fileSize = typeof fileStat.size === 'bigint' ? Number(fileStat.size) : fileStat.size
   66        const revision = `${String(fileStat.size)}:${String(fileStat.mtimeMs)}`
   67        const expectedRevision = typeof args.expected_revision === 'string' ? args.expected_revision : undefined
   68        if (expectedRevision && expectedRevision !== revision) {
   69          return {
   70            output: {
   71              code: 'file_revision_mismatch',
   72              error: 'file changed since the previous page; restart the read without expected_revision',
   73              path: absolutePath,
   74              relative_path: relativePath,
   75              expected_revision: expectedRevision,
   76              revision
   77            },
   78            isError: true
   79          }
   80        }
   81        if (maxFileBytes !== undefined && typeof fileStat.size === 'number' && fileStat.size > maxFileBytes) {
   82          return {
   83            output: {
   84              code: 'file_too_large',
   85              error: `refusing to read ${formatSize(fileStat.size)} file (maximum ${formatSize(maxFileBytes)})`,
   86              path: absolutePath,
   87              relative_path: relativePath,
   88              byte_size: fileStat.size,
   89              max_file_bytes: maxFileBytes,
