源码 ↗

range — instead of forcing every big payload into context. Read-only.

read_artifact→解析→路由→策略→处理器→回执
展开逐行源码证据
   10  import { LocalToolHost, type LocalTool } from './local-tool-host.js'
   11  import { readArtifactBounded } from '../../artifacts/artifact-store.js'
   12
   13  export function createReadArtifactTool(): LocalTool {
   14    return LocalToolHost.defineTool({
   15      name: 'read_artifact',
   16      description:
   17        'Read a stored artifact (large tool output) by id. Optionally fetch only a slice: ' +
   18        'startLine/endLine for a 1-indexed inclusive line range, or offset/length for a UTF-8 byte range. ' +
   19        'Use the artifact id returned by a previous tool result (e.g. stdoutArtifactId).',
   20      inputSchema: {
   21        type: 'object',
   22        properties: {
   23          artifactId: { type: 'string' },
   24          offset: { type: 'number' },
   25          length: { type: 'number' },
   26          startLine: { type: 'number' },
   27          endLine: { type: 'number' }
   28        },
   29        required: ['artifactId'],
   30        additionalProperties: false
   31      },
   32      policy: 'auto',
   33      execute: async (args, context) => {
   34        if (!context.artifactStore) {
   35          return { output: { error: 'artifact store is not available in this runtime' }, isError: true }
   36        }
   37        const artifactId = typeof args.artifactId === 'string' ? args.artifactId.trim() : ''
   38        if (!artifactId) return { output: { error: 'artifactId is required' }, isError: true }
   39        if (
   40          context.allowedArtifactIds &&
   41          !context.allowedArtifactIds.includes(artifactId)
   42        ) {
   43          return {
   44            output: { error: `artifact is outside the delegated child capability set: ${artifactId}` },
   45            isError: true
   46          }
   47        }
   48        const meta = await context.artifactStore.stat(artifactId)
   49        if (!meta) return { output: { error: `artifact not found: ${artifactId}` }, isError: true }
   50        const range = {
   51          ...(typeof args.offset === 'number' ? { offset: args.offset } : {}),
   52          ...(typeof args.length === 'number' ? { length: args.length } : {}),
   53          ...(typeof args.startLine === 'number' ? { startLine: args.startLine } : {}),
   54          ...(typeof args.endLine === 'number' ? { endLine: args.endLine } : {})
   55        }
   56        // Always read through the bounded reader: a request with no range, or a
   57        // range larger than the cap, is clamped to <=1 MiB / <=2000 lines and a
   58        // cursor is returned so the model pages rather than pulling a huge result
   59        // into context.
   60        const bounded = await readArtifactBounded(context.artifactStore, artifactId, meta, range)
   61        if (bounded === null) return { output: { error: `artifact content not found: ${artifactId}` }, isError: true }
   62        return {
   63          output: {
   64            artifactId,
   65            byteSize: meta.byteSize,
   66            lineCount: meta.lineCount,
   67            ...(meta.source ? { source: meta.source } : {}),
   68            ...(meta.origin ? { origin: meta.origin } : {}),
   69            range: bounded.range,
   70            truncated: bounded.truncated,
   71            ...(bounded.nextOffset !== undefined ? { nextOffset: bounded.nextOffset } : {}),
   72            ...(bounded.nextStartLine !== undefined ? { nextStartLine: bounded.nextStartLine } : {}),
   73            content: bounded.content
   74          }

task_graph→解析→路由→策略→处理器→回执

   47      runnable: graph.nextRunnable().map((t) => t.id),

   49    })
   50
   51    return LocalToolHost.defineTool({
   52      name: 'task_graph',
   53      description:
   54        'Plan and drive a dependency-aware task graph for this thread. actions: ' +
   55        '"add" (id,title,dependsOn?,priority?), "list", "next" (runnable now), ' +
   56        '"start" (id), "complete" (id), "fail" (id,error), and "pause"/"resume"/"cancel" (id). ' +
   57        'Retry and concurrency policy are configured by the host. Tasks become runnable only when their dependencies succeed.',
   58      inputSchema: {
   59        type: 'object',
   60        properties: {
   61          action: { type: 'string', enum: ['add', 'list', 'next', 'start', 'complete', 'fail', 'pause', 'resume', 'cancel'] },
   62          id: { type: 'string' },
   63          title: { type: 'string' },
   64          dependsOn: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'string' } },
   65          priority: { type: 'number' },
   66          error: { type: 'string' }
   67        },
   68        required: ['action'],
   69        additionalProperties: false
   70      },
   71      policy: 'auto',
   72      execute: async (args, context) => {
   73        const graph = await graphFor(context.threadId)
   74        const action = typeof args.action === 'string' ? args.action : ''
   75        const id = typeof args.id === 'string' ? args.id.trim() : ''
   76        try {
   77          switch (action) {
   78            case 'add': {
   79              if (!id || typeof args.title !== 'string' || !args.title.trim()) {
   80                return { output: { error: 'id and title are required for add' }, isError: true }
   81              }
   82              graph.add({
   83                id,
   84                title: args.title.trim(),
   85                ...(Array.isArray(args.dependsOn) ? { dependsOn: args.dependsOn.filter((d): d is string => typeof d === 'string') } : {}),
   86                ...(typeof args.priority === 'number' ? { priority: args.priority } : {}),
   87                maxAttempts
   88              })
   89              await save(context.threadId, graph)
   90              return { output: { action, added: id, ...snapshot(graph) } }
   91            }
   92            case 'list':
   93            case 'next':
   94              return { output: { action, ...snapshot(graph) } }
   95            case 'start':
   96              graph.markRunning(id)
   97              await save(context.threadId, graph)
   98              return { output: { action, id, ...snapshot(graph) } }
   99            case 'complete':
  100              graph.markSucceeded(id)
  101              await save(context.threadId, graph)
  102              return { output: { action, id, ...snapshot(graph) } }
  103            case 'fail': {
  104              const outcome = graph.markFailed(id, typeof args.error === 'string' ? args.error : 'unspecified failure')
  105              await save(context.threadId, graph)
  106              return { output: { action, id, retried: outcome.retried, ...snapshot(graph) } }
  107            }
  108            case 'pause': graph.pause(id); await save(context.threadId, graph); return { output: { action, id, ...snapshot(graph) } }
  109            case 'resume': graph.resume(id); await save(context.threadId, graph); return { output: { action, id, ...snapshot(graph) } }
  110            case 'cancel': graph.cancel(id); await save(context.threadId, graph); return { output: { action, id, ...snapshot(graph) } }
  111            default:

  999      const signal = withTimeout(request.signal, request.timeoutMs)
 1000      let response: Response
 1001      try {
 1002        response = await fetch(this.endpointUrl, {
 1003          method: 'POST',
 1004          headers: {
 1005            Authorization: `Bearer ${this.apiKey}`,
 1006            'Content-Type': 'application/json'
 1007          },
 1008          body: JSON.stringify({
 1009            model: request.model,
 1010            prompt: request.prompt,
 1011            ...(images.length > 0 ? { image: images.map(imageDataUrl) } : {}),
 1012            ...(request.size ? { size: request.size } : { size: '2K' }),
 1013            output_format: 'png',
 1014            response_format: 'b64_json',
 1015            sequential_image_generation: 'disabled',
 1016            stream: false,
 1017            watermark: false
 1018          }),
 1019          signal
 1020        })
 1021      } catch (error) {
 1022        throw imageFetchFailure(this.endpointUrl, error, request)
 1023      }
 1024      if (!response.ok) {
 1025        throw new ImageGenHttpError(response.status, await response.text())
 1026      }
 1027      const payload = (await response.json()) as VolcengineArkImagesPayload
 1028      const entry = payload.data?.[0]
 1029      if (entry?.b64_json) {
 1030        return { data: Buffer.from(entry.b64_json, 'base64'), mimeType: 'image/png' }
 1031      }
 1032      if (entry?.url) {
 1033        let download: Response
 1034        try {
 1035          download = await fetch(entry.url, { signal })

web_fetch

模型可调用的内置工具 · tool registry · kun/src/adapters/tool/web-tool-provider.ts:138
源码 ↗

Fetch an allowed HTTP or HTTPS URL and return extracted text with source metadata.

web_fetch→解析→路由→策略→处理器→回执
展开逐行源码证据
  133    }
  134  }
  135
  136  function createFetchTool(config: WebCapabilityConfig, provider: WebProvider) {
  137    return LocalToolHost.defineTool({
  138      name: 'web_fetch',
  139      description: 'Fetch an allowed HTTP or HTTPS URL and return extracted text with source metadata.',
  140      inputSchema: {
  141        type: 'object',
  142        properties: {
  143          url: { type: 'string' },
  144          max_bytes: { type: 'number' }
  145        },
  146        required: ['url'],
  147        additionalProperties: false
  148      },
  149      policy: 'untrusted',
  150      execute: async (args, context) => {
  151        const startedAt = Date.now()
  152        const rawUrl = pickString(args.url)
  153        if (!rawUrl) return toolError('invalid_url', 'url is required')
  154        const policy = validateUrlPolicy(rawUrl, config)
  155        if (!policy.ok) return toolError('policy_blocked', policy.reason, telemetry({ startedAt, policy: 'blocked', url: rawUrl }))
  156        if (!provider.fetch) return toolError('provider_unavailable', 'web fetch provider is unavailable')
  157        const maxBytesCap = config.maxFetchBytes ?? DEFAULT_WEB_MAX_BYTES
  158        const maxBytes = boundedInt(
  159          args.max_bytes,
  160          maxBytesCap,
  161          Math.min(MIN_WEB_FETCH_BYTES, maxBytesCap),
  162          maxBytesCap
  163        )
  164        try {
  165          const result = await provider.fetch({
  166            url: policy.url.href,
  167            maxBytes,
  168            timeoutMs: WEB_REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS,
  169            signal: context.abortSignal
  170          })
  171          return {
  172            output: fetchOutput(result, telemetry({
  173              startedAt,
  174              policy: 'allowed',
  175              url: policy.url.href,
  176              provider: provider.id,
  177              byteCount: result.byteCount
  178            }))
  179          }
  180        } catch (error) {
  181          if (error instanceof WebFetchPolicyError) {
  182            return toolError('policy_blocked', error.message, telemetry({
  183              startedAt,
  184              policy: 'blocked',
  185              url: policy.url.href,
  186              provider: provider.id
  187            }))
  188          }
  189          return toolError('fetch_failed', errorMessage(error), telemetry({
  190            startedAt,
  191            policy: 'allowed',
  192            url: policy.url.href,
  193            provider: provider.id
  194          }))
  195        }
  196      }
  197    })

  197    })

  199
  200  function createSearchTool(config: WebCapabilityConfig, provider: WebProvider) {
  201    return LocalToolHost.defineTool({
  202      name: 'web_search',
  203      description: 'Search the web through the configured provider and return ranked results with source metadata.',
  204      inputSchema: {
  205        type: 'object',
  206        properties: {
  207          query: { type: 'string' },
  208          limit: { type: 'number' }
  209        },
  210        required: ['query'],
  211        additionalProperties: false
  212      },
  213      policy: 'untrusted',
  214      execute: async (args, context) => {
  215        const startedAt = Date.now()
  216        const query = pickString(args.query)
  217        if (!query) return toolError('invalid_query', 'query is required')
  218        if (!provider.search) return toolError('provider_unavailable', 'web search provider is unavailable')
  219        const limit = boundedInt(args.limit, DEFAULT_SEARCH_LIMIT, 1, MAX_SEARCH_LIMIT)
  220        try {
  221          const results = await provider.search({
  222            query,
  223            limit,
  224            timeoutMs: WEB_REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS,
  225            signal: context.abortSignal
  226          })
  227          return {
  228            output: searchOutput(query, provider.id, results, telemetry({
  229              startedAt,
  230              policy: 'allowed',
  231              provider: provider.id,
  232              query,
  233              resultCount: results.length
  234            }))
  235          }
  236        } catch (error) {
  237          return toolError('search_failed', errorMessage(error), telemetry({
  238            startedAt,
  239            policy: 'allowed',
  240            provider: provider.id,
  241            query
  242          }))
  243        }
  244      }
  245    })
  246  }
  247
  248  export class FetchWebProvider implements WebProvider {
  249    readonly id = 'fetch'
  250    private readonly config: WebCapabilityConfig
  251    private readonly nowIso: () => string
  252    private readonly resolveHost: (hostname: string) => Promise<ResolvedAddress[]>
  253    private readonly request: (request: FetchWebTransportRequest) => Promise<FetchWebTransportResponse>
  254
  255    constructor(config: WebCapabilityConfig, options: FetchWebProviderOptions = {}) {
  256      this.config = config
  257      this.nowIso = options.nowIso ?? (() => new Date().toISOString())
  258      this.resolveHost = options.resolveHost ?? resolveHostAddresses
  259      this.request = options.request ?? requestWithPinnedLookup
  260    }

展开逐行源码证据
  114  export function createWriteLocalTool(_options: WriteLocalToolOptions = {}): LocalTool {
  115    const mkdirOp = _options.operations?.mkdir ?? defaultWriteLocalToolOperations.mkdir!
  116    const writeFileOp = _options.operations?.writeFile ?? defaultWriteLocalToolOperations.writeFile!
  117    const openExternalOp = _options.operations?.openExternal ?? open
  118    return LocalToolHost.defineTool({
  119      name: 'write',
  120      description: 'Create or overwrite a workspace file with the provided content.',
  121      inputSchema: {
  122        type: 'object',
  123        properties: {
  124          path: { type: 'string' },
  125          content: { type: 'string' }
  126        },
  127        required: ['path', 'content'],
  128        additionalProperties: false
  129      },
  130      policy: 'on-request',
  131      toolKind: 'file_change',
  132      externalWritePathArguments: ['path'],
  133      execute: async (args, context) => withToolBoundary(async () => {
  134        const truncated = truncatedArgumentsError(args.__raw)
  135        if (truncated) return truncated
  136        const rawPath = typeof args.path === 'string' ? args.path : ''
  137        const content = typeof args.content === 'string' ? args.content : null
  138        if (!rawPath.trim() || content == null) {
  139          return { output: { error: 'path and content are required' }, isError: true }
  140        }
  141        const { absolutePath, relativePath } = await resolveWorkspacePath(rawPath, context)
  142        assertCanWritePath(absolutePath, context)
  143        return withFileMutationQueue(absolutePath, async () => {
  144          const externalTarget = approvedExternalTarget(absolutePath, context)
  145          if (externalTarget) {
  146            const handle = await openVerifiedExternalTarget(externalTarget, 'write', openExternalOp)
  147            try {
  148              await writeTextToHandle(handle, content, externalTarget)
  149            } finally {
  150              await handle.close()
  151            }
  152          } else {
  153            await mkdirOp(dirname(absolutePath))
  154            await writeFileOp(absolutePath, content)
  155          }
  156          return {
  157            output: {
  158              path: absolutePath,
  159              relative_path: relativePath,
  160              bytes_written: Buffer.byteLength(content, 'utf8')
  161            }
  162          }
  163        })
  164      })
  165    })
  166  }
  167
  168  export const createWriteTool = createWriteLocalTool
  169  export const createWriteToolDefinition = createWriteLocalTool

  173    const writeFileOp = _options.operations?.writeFile ?? defaultEditLocalToolOperations.writeFile!
  174    const openExternalOp = _options.operations?.openExternal ?? open
  175    return LocalToolHost.defineTool({
  176      name: 'edit',
  177      description: 'Edit a workspace file using exact text replacement. Supports multiple disjoint edits in one call.',
  178      inputSchema: {

  137    general: GENERAL_PROFILE,
  138    explore: EXPLORE_PROFILE,
  139    'component-designer': COMPONENT_DESIGNER_PROFILE,
  140    'design-reviewer': DESIGN_REVIEWER_PROFILE,
  141    'over-engineering-reviewer': OVER_ENGINEERING_REVIEWER_PROFILE,
  142    ...AGENT_SKILLS_SUBAGENT_PROFILES,
  143    ...WORKFLOW_SUBAGENT_PROFILES,
  144    ...SURFACE_SPECIALIST_SUBAGENT_PROFILES
  145  }
  146
  147  /** All builtin profiles, keyed by their `delegate_task` profile name. */
  148  export const BUILTIN_SUBAGENT_PROFILES: Readonly<Record<string, SubagentProfileConfig>> =
  149    Object.fromEntries(BUILTIN_AGENT_CATALOG.map((metadata) => {
  150      const profile = BUILTIN_SUBAGENT_PROFILE_BASES[metadata.id]
  151      if (!profile) throw new Error(`missing built-in subagent definition: ${metadata.id}`)
  152      return [metadata.id, {
  153        ...profile,
  154        name: metadata.name,
  155        description: metadata.description,
  156        color: metadata.color,
  157        toolPolicy: metadata.toolPolicy,
  158        surfaces: [...metadata.surfaces]
  159      } satisfies SubagentProfileConfig]
  160    }))
  161
  162  /** Merge builtin profiles into a subagents config (user profiles take precedence). */
  163  export function mergeBuiltinSubagentProfiles(
  164    config: SubagentsCapabilityConfig
  165  ): SubagentsCapabilityConfig {
  166    // Per-id DEEP merge (builtin base < user override), NOT a shallow replace.
  167    // The GUI persists a builtin override carrying only the edited fields (a
  168    // model pick, a reasoning level, or a deny-list) and drops the localized
  169    // name; a shallow `{ ...builtins, ...config.profiles }` would let that thin
  170    // override clobber the builtin's promptPreamble/description/systemPrompt.
  171    // Merging per id keeps those as fallbacks while the user's fields still win.
  172    const profiles: Record<string, SubagentProfileConfig> = Object.fromEntries(
  173      Object.entries(config.profiles).map(([id, profile]) => [id, canonicalSurfaceProfile(profile)])
  174    )
  175    for (const [id, builtin] of Object.entries(BUILTIN_SUBAGENT_PROFILES)) {
  176      const override = config.profiles[id]
  177      profiles[id] = canonicalSurfaceProfile(override ? { ...builtin, ...override } : builtin)
  178    }
  179    profiles.general = { ...profiles.general, surfaces: ['shared'] }
  180    // Default a child with no explicit `profile` to the built-in `general`
  181    // profile (always present after the merge). Without this, an omitted profile
  182    // resolves to `undefined`, so the run carries no profile id — the GUI then
  183    // can't label the subagent and falls back to a generic name.
  184    const defaultProfile = config.defaultProfile ?? 'general'
  185    return { ...config, profiles, defaultProfile }
  186  }
  187
  188  function canonicalSurfaceProfile(profile: SubagentProfileConfig): SubagentProfileConfig {
  189    const surfaces = profile.surfaces ?? ['shared']
打开这条指令的动态执行图 →

  288      case 'btw': return rest
  289        ? { kind: 'btw', question: rest }
  290        : { kind: 'command-usage', usage: '/btw <question>' }
  291      case 'context': return { kind: 'context' }
  292      case 'capabilities': return { kind: 'capabilities' }
  293      case 'queue': return { kind: 'queue', ...(rest ? { action: rest } : {}) }
  294      case 'q':
  295      case 'exit':
  296      case 'quit': return { kind: 'quit' }
  297      default: {
  298        if (name.startsWith('skill:')) {
  299          const skillName = name.slice('skill:'.length).trim()
  300          return skillName
  301            ? { kind: 'skill', name: skillName, ...(rest ? { prompt: rest } : {}) }
  302            : { kind: 'command-usage', usage: '/skill:<name> [prompt]' }
  303        }
  304        return { kind: 'unknown', name }
  305      }
  306    }
  307  }
  308
  309  const GRAPH_COMMAND_ACTIONS = new Set([

  292      case 'capabilities': return { kind: 'capabilities' }

  294      case 'q':
  295      case 'exit':
  296      case 'quit': return { kind: 'quit' }
  297      default: {

  300          return skillName
  301            ? { kind: 'skill', name: skillName, ...(rest ? { prompt: rest } : {}) }

  303        }
  304        return { kind: 'unknown', name }
  305      }

/paste

交互 slash 指令 · dispatch switch · kun/src/tui/commands.ts:259
源码 ↗

case 'memory':

/paste→解析→路由→策略→处理器→回执
展开逐行源码证据
  254      case 'subagents': return { kind: 'subagents', ...(rest ? { action: rest } : {}) }
  255      case 'tasks':
  256      case 'todo':
  257      case 'todos': return { kind: 'tasks', ...(rest ? { action: rest } : {}) }
  258      case 'attach': return { kind: 'attach', ...(rest ? { path: rest } : {}) }
  259      case 'paste': return { kind: 'paste' }
  260      case 'memory':
  261      case 'memories': return { kind: 'memory', ...(rest ? { action: rest } : {}) }
  262      case 'shells':
  263      case 'jobs': return { kind: 'shells', ...(rest ? { action: rest } : {}) }
  264      case 'extensions':
  265      case 'plugins': return { kind: 'extensions', ...(rest ? { action: rest } : {}) }
  266      case 'theme': return { kind: 'theme', ...(rest ? { name: rest } : {}) }
  267      case 'share': return { kind: 'share' }
  268      case 'unshare': return { kind: 'unshare' }
  269      case 'console': return { kind: 'console' }
  270      case 'diff': return { kind: 'diff' }
  271      case 'terminal': return { kind: 'terminal' }
  272      case 'update': return { kind: 'update', confirm: rest.toLowerCase() === 'yes' }
  273      case 'plan': return { kind: 'plan', ...(rest ? { action: rest } : {}) }
  274      case 'graph': {
  275        if (!rest) return { kind: 'graph' }
  276        const action = rest.toLowerCase()
  277        return GRAPH_COMMAND_ACTIONS.has(action)
  278          ? { kind: 'graph', action }
  279          : { kind: 'graph', prompt: rest }
  280      }
  281      case 'agent': return { kind: 'agent' }
  282      case 'goal': return { kind: 'goal', ...(rest ? { action: rest } : {}) }
  283      case 'skills': return { kind: 'skills', ...(rest ? { query: rest } : {}) }
  284      case 'editor': return { kind: 'editor', ...(rest ? { initial: rest } : {}) }
  285      case 'add-dir': return rest
  286        ? { kind: 'add-dir', path: rest }
  287        : { kind: 'command-usage', usage: '/add-dir <path>' }
  288      case 'btw': return rest
  289        ? { kind: 'btw', question: rest }
  290        : { kind: 'command-usage', usage: '/btw <question>' }
  291      case 'context': return { kind: 'context' }
  292      case 'capabilities': return { kind: 'capabilities' }
  293      case 'queue': return { kind: 'queue', ...(rest ? { action: rest } : {}) }
  294      case 'q':
  295      case 'exit':
  296      case 'quit': return { kind: 'quit' }
  297      default: {
  298        if (name.startsWith('skill:')) {
  299          const skillName = name.slice('skill:'.length).trim()
  300          return skillName
  301            ? { kind: 'skill', name: skillName, ...(rest ? { prompt: rest } : {}) }
  302            : { kind: 'command-usage', usage: '/skill:<name> [prompt]' }
  303        }
  304        return { kind: 'unknown', name }
  305      }
  306    }
  307  }
  308
  309  const GRAPH_COMMAND_ACTIONS = new Set([
  310    'status',
  311    'list',
  312    'off',
  313    'direct',
  314    'agent',
  315    'on',
  316    'start'
  317  ])

  275        if (!rest) return { kind: 'graph' }
  276        const action = rest.toLowerCase()
  277        return GRAPH_COMMAND_ACTIONS.has(action)
  278          ? { kind: 'graph', action }
  279          : { kind: 'graph', prompt: rest }
  280      }
  281      case 'agent': return { kind: 'agent' }
  282      case 'goal': return { kind: 'goal', ...(rest ? { action: rest } : {}) }
  283      case 'skills': return { kind: 'skills', ...(rest ? { query: rest } : {}) }
  284      case 'editor': return { kind: 'editor', ...(rest ? { initial: rest } : {}) }
  285      case 'add-dir': return rest
  286        ? { kind: 'add-dir', path: rest }
  287        : { kind: 'command-usage', usage: '/add-dir <path>' }
  288      case 'btw': return rest
  289        ? { kind: 'btw', question: rest }
  290        : { kind: 'command-usage', usage: '/btw <question>' }
  291      case 'context': return { kind: 'context' }
  292      case 'capabilities': return { kind: 'capabilities' }
  293      case 'queue': return { kind: 'queue', ...(rest ? { action: rest } : {}) }
  294      case 'q':
  295      case 'exit':
  296      case 'quit': return { kind: 'quit' }
  297      default: {
  298        if (name.startsWith('skill:')) {
  299          const skillName = name.slice('skill:'.length).trim()
  300          return skillName
  301            ? { kind: 'skill', name: skillName, ...(rest ? { prompt: rest } : {}) }
  302            : { kind: 'command-usage', usage: '/skill:<name> [prompt]' }
  303        }
  304        return { kind: 'unknown', name }
  305      }
  306    }
  307  }
  308
  309  const GRAPH_COMMAND_ACTIONS = new Set([
  310    'status',
  311    'list',
  312    'off',
  313    'direct',
  314    'agent',
  315    'on',
  316    'start'
  317  ])
  318

交互层可直接触发的 slash 指令；本卡展示它的注册点和进入处理器后的执行边界。

  262      case 'shells':
  263      case 'jobs': return { kind: 'shells', ...(rest ? { action: rest } : {}) }
  264      case 'extensions':
  265      case 'plugins': return { kind: 'extensions', ...(rest ? { action: rest } : {}) }
  266      case 'theme': return { kind: 'theme', ...(rest ? { name: rest } : {}) }
  267      case 'share': return { kind: 'share' }
  268      case 'unshare': return { kind: 'unshare' }
  269      case 'console': return { kind: 'console' }
  270      case 'diff': return { kind: 'diff' }
  271      case 'terminal': return { kind: 'terminal' }
  272      case 'update': return { kind: 'update', confirm: rest.toLowerCase() === 'yes' }

  297      default: {

  300          return skillName
  301            ? { kind: 'skill', name: skillName, ...(rest ? { prompt: rest } : {}) }
  302            : { kind: 'command-usage', usage: '/skill:<name> [prompt]' }
  303        }

  305      }

四问：从代码追到执行语义

COMMANDS · 四个实现问题
ENTRY

入口 / 注册

先定位谁创建这次运行、注册了哪些能力，以及输入怎样进入状态。

Direct mode 与 Agent Graph 共享 workflow runtime；extension-api 把 Agent、Tool、Permission、RunEvent 对外固化成 schema。
docs/workflow-loop.md:1–25 ↗
STATE

状态 / 投影

再找会话、上下文、队列或 projection 的所有权；状态不是 UI 文案。

ToolInvocation、progress、result 和 cancellation token 形成工具回执协议，工具不只是一个返回字符串的函数。
packages/extension-api/src/tools.ts:13–42 ↗
EFFECT

操作 / 事务

把模型输出、工具执行、权限检查和外部副作用拆开，确认有没有回执。

把 Kun 想成一张任务白板：每个节点都有输入、权限、状态和交付物，线条表示谁把结果交给谁。
packages/extension-api/src/agent.ts:11–40 ↗
RECOVERY

失败 / 恢复

最后追踪重试、压缩、取消、回滚、持久化或人工接管出口。

AgentProfileDeclaration、extension visibility、tools.register 和权限声明共同构成插件/指令面，调用者必须声明需要什么。
packages/extension-api/src/agent.ts:11–40 ↗

4. 与“内置指令全景：从注册到执行回执”直接相关的源码入口；先看这一段的输入、状态和返回值。

    1  export type GuiPlanCommand =
    2    | { kind: 'open' }
    3    | { kind: 'create'; request: string }
    4
    5  export function parseGuiPlanCommand(input: string): GuiPlanCommand | null {
    6    const trimmed = input.trim()
    7    if (!trimmed.startsWith('/plan')) return null
    8    const next = trimmed.at('/plan'.length)
    9    if (next && !/\s/.test(next)) return null
   10    const request = trimmed.slice('/plan'.length).trim()
   11    return request ? { kind: 'create', request } : { kind: 'open' }
   12  }
   13

   20      const service = await projectService()

   22      const manual = { ...structuredClone(initial), name: 'Manual won' }
   23      const agent = { ...structuredClone(initial), name: 'Agent won' }
   24
   25      const settled = await Promise.allSettled([
   26        service.executeCommand({
   27          projectId: initial.id,
   28          expectedRevision: 0,
   29          attribution: { author: 'manual', sourceOperation: 'ui.rename', summary: 'Manual rename' },
   30          command: { kind: 'replace-project', project: manual }
   31        }),
   32        service.executeCommand({
   33          projectId: initial.id,
   34          expectedRevision: 0,
   35          attribution: {
   36            author: 'agent',
   37            actorId: 'main-agent',
   38            sourceOperation: 'agent.rename',
   39            summary: 'Agent rename'
   40          },
   41          command: { kind: 'replace-project', project: agent }
   42        })
   43      ])
   44
   45      expect(settled.filter(({ status }) => status === 'fulfilled')).toHaveLength(1)
   46      const rejected = settled.find(({ status }) => status === 'rejected')
   47      expect(rejected).toMatchObject({ reason: { code: 'revision_conflict' } })
   48      const loaded = await service.loadProject(initial.id)
   49      expect(loaded.currentRevision).toBe(1)
   50      expect(['Manual won', 'Agent won']).toContain(loaded.name)

  169  function runFfmpeg(args: string[], timeout = 60_000): void {
  170    const result = spawnSync(ffmpeg, args, {
  171      encoding: 'utf8',
  172      timeout,
  173      maxBuffer: 10 * 1024 * 1024
  174    })
  175    if (result.status !== 0) {
  176      throw new Error(
  177        `FFmpeg failed (status=${String(result.status)}, signal=${String(result.signal)}):\n` +
  178        `${result.stderr || result.stdout}`
  179      )
  180    }
  181  }
  182
  183  function probeDurationSeconds(file: string): number {
  184    const result = spawnSync(ffmpeg, ['-hide_banner', '-i', file], {
  185      encoding: 'utf8',
  186      timeout: 10_000
  187    })
  188    const match = /Duration: (\d{2}):(\d{2}):(\d{2}\.\d+)/u.exec(result.stderr)
  189    if (!match) throw new Error(`FFmpeg did not report a duration for ${file}:\n${result.stderr}`)
  190    return Number(match[1]) * 3600 + Number(match[2]) * 60 + Number(match[3])
  191  }
